### [1] [ELECTRIC: Evidential Learning-Enhanced CT Reconstruction via Iterative Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00060)
*Ge Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ELECTRIC框架，将证据学习（evidential learning）与物理引导的迭代校正相结合，用于低剂量CT图像重建，通过贝叶斯公式和自适应精度场提升重建质量与不确定性估计。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2分）。论文属于医学影像（CT）重建领域，与遥感、卫星、在轨部署、目标检测均无直接关联。虽然涉及不确定性估计和迭代优化，但场景是医学CT图像重建，不是遥感场景理解或目标检测，也不涉及任何轻量化/部署方法。属于泛机器学习/医学影像方法，对遥感目标检测轻量化课题的借鉴价值有限。

Reading priority: 1分。论文属于医学CT重建领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不涉及目标检测、遥感场景或任何模型压缩/部署方法，阅读优先级极低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：低剂量CT图像重建（Low-Dose CT Reconstruction）；应用场景：医学影像；传感器类型：CT扫描仪（非遥感传感器）；目标类别：不涉及目标检测，处理的是医学图像切片。

Model architecture: ELECTRIC框架包含：1) 证据神经网络（evidential neural network）提供图像proposal和误差预测的epistemic-uncertainty surrogate；2) 自适应精度场（adaptive precision field）插入到Poisson-weighted MAP更新中；3) image-evidence-precision-reconstruction闭环迭代结构，将先验置信度作为迭代重建的学习状态变量。使用Normal-Inverse-Gamma证据网络驱动闭环。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等任何模型压缩或加速方法，也不涉及实时推理或边缘部署。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署相关信息，且场景为医学CT重建，与星上部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：AAPM Mayo Clinic Low-Dose CT dataset（低剂量CT数据集）；评价指标：重建误差（reconstruction error，相对filtered back-projection降低约70%）、epistemic uncertainty的误差预测能力（error-predictive）、measurement consistency。实验包括两项仿真研究：机制验证（mechanism-validation pilot）和可行性研究（feasibility study）。

Experiments: 在held-out患者上：1) 学习到的先验均值相对filtered back-projection将重建误差降低约70%；2) 学习到的epistemic uncertainty具有误差预测能力并支持选择性信任（selective trust）；3) 物理引导更新恢复测量一致性；4) 自适应精度重建匹配或超过validation-tuned固定先验，且对先验强度误设定（prior-strength misspecification）更鲁棒。

Limitations: 摘要明确指出未来工作方向为形式化不确定性校准（formal uncertainty calibration）和联合训练（joint training）。此外：1) 与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化完全无关；2) 仅在仿真研究上验证，未涉及真实临床部署；3) 未报告计算开销和推理效率。

Ideas for my research: 尽管场景不同，但以下方法论思想可迁移到遥感在轨目标检测：1) 证据学习（evidential learning）与epistemic uncertainty估计可用于遥感目标检测中的不确定性感知推理，帮助在轨系统判断检测结果的可信度，实现选择性信任（selective trust）；2) 物理引导（physics-guided）的迭代校正思想可类比为遥感成像物理模型（如大气校正、传感器噪声模型）引导的检测后处理；3) 自适应精度/置信度作为可学习状态变量的闭环思想，可用于在轨检测模型的在线自适应更新。但整体借鉴价值有限，因为论文未涉及检测任务和轻量化部署。

Abstract: Here we introduce ELECTRIC (Evidential Learning-Enhanced CT Reconstruction via Iterative Correction), a physics-guided Bayesian formulation. An evidential neural network provides an image proposal and an error-predictive epistemic-uncertainty surrogate. The latter is converted into an adaptive precision field and inserted into a Poisson-weighted MAP update. The resulting image-evidence-precision-reconstruction loop treats prior confidence as a learned state variable of iterative reconstruction. In addition to the formulation and theoretical analysis, we report two simulation studies on image slices from the AAPM Mayo Clinic Low-Dose CT dataset: a mechanism-validation pilot using transparent surrogate estimators, and a feasibility study in which a trained Normal-Inverse-Gamma evidential network drives the full closed loop. On held-out patients, the learned prior mean reduces reconstruction error by roughly 70 percent relative to filtered back-projection, the learned epistemic uncertainty is error-predictive and supports selective trust, and the physics-guided update restores measurement consistency while the adaptive-precision reconstruction matches or exceeds a validation-tuned fixed prior and remains markedly more robust to prior-strength misspecification. Together these results demonstrate the complete ELECTRIC closed-loop pipeline, while identifying formal uncertainty calibration and joint training as the principal directions for future work.

</details>


### [2] [Noise-Robust Conditional Flow Matching: Generating Clean Samples from Noisy Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00064)
*Adrian Urbański,Gabriel della Maggiora,Artur Yakimovich*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Noise-Robust Conditional Flow Matching (NR-CFM)，一种能从单张含噪观测中学习干净数据分布的无条件生成模型，用于科学成像中的噪声鲁棒生成。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2分）。论文属于生成模型/科学成像领域，与遥感、卫星、在轨部署、目标检测、轻量化均无直接关联。虽然"从噪声观测中学习干净分布"的思路在遥感图像去噪/复原场景中可能有间接借鉴意义，但论文本身不涉及遥感场景、目标检测任务或任何模型压缩/轻量化方法。

Reading priority: 1分。论文聚焦科学成像中的噪声鲁棒生成建模，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不涉及目标检测、遥感场景或任何轻量化/部署技术，仅可作为遥感图像去噪方向的间接背景参考。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：无条件图像生成（unconditional generation）与噪声鲁棒生成建模；应用场景：科学成像（scientific imaging），如粒子图像（particle images）；传感器类型：摘要未说明（非遥感传感器）；目标类别：不涉及目标检测，生成对象为科学粒子图像。

Model architecture: 基于 Conditional Flow Matching (CFM) 框架，引入噪声鲁棒修正机制：对加性高斯白噪声（additive white Gaussian noise）提供闭式干净端点修正（closed-form clean endpoint correction），对更复杂协方差结构的一般高斯噪声学习数据驱动的修正。整体为无条件生成器（unconditional generator），从每张图像的单次含噪观测中学习。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署相关信息。且生成模型（flow matching）通常计算开销较大，不适合星上实时推理场景。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未明确列出具体数据集名称。评估设置包括：多种噪声破坏设置（corruption settings），对比方法为 NR-GAN 和 Ambient Diffusion；在信噪比（SNR）低至 0.001 的科学数据上评估，生成合理的粒子图像。具体指标（如 FID、IS 等）摘要未说明。

Experiments: 在多种噪声设置下，NR-CFM 在大多数情况下优于 NR-GAN，在高噪声区间与 Ambient Diffusion 表现相当；在 SNR 低至 0.001 的极端噪声条件下仍能生成合理的粒子图像。具体数值结果摘要未说明。

Limitations: 1) 与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化方向无直接关联；2) 仅针对高斯噪声建模，对遥感图像中常见的斑点噪声（SAR）、条带噪声、云遮挡等复杂退化未涉及；3) 生成模型推理成本高，未讨论部署可行性；4) 摘要未提供定量指标和数据集细节。

Ideas for my research: 1) NR-CFM 中"从含噪观测学习干净分布"的思想可迁移到遥感图像去噪/复原任务，作为目标检测前的预处理模块，提升低信噪比遥感图像中的检测精度；2) flow matching 的稳定训练和高效采样特性值得关注，若未来结合轻量化蒸馏（如将生成模型蒸馏为轻量网络）可能对星上图像增强有启发；3) 但整体借鉴价值有限，建议仅作为背景阅读。

Abstract: Generative models learn the statistical properties of their training data, so high-quality generation depends on clean and representative datasets. In scientific imaging, acquisition often yields noisy measurements, while collecting clean references can be costly, impractical or even unattainable. Training directly on these measurements results in a model that reproduces the corrupted data. This can be circumvented by learning the clean population distribution directly from the noisy data. Conditional flow matching (CFM) combines a simple regression objective with stable training, efficient sampling, and strong image-generation performance, making it a natural framework for this setting. We introduce Noise-Robust Conditional Flow Matching (NR-CFM), an unconditional generator that learns from one corrupted observation per image. NR-CFM provides a closed-form clean endpoint correction for additive white Gaussian noise and learns a data-driven correction for general Gaussian corruptions with more complex covariance structure. Across the evaluated corruption settings, NR-CFM outperforms NR-GAN in most cases and remains competitive with Ambient Diffusion in the high-noise regime. We further evaluate NR-CFM on scientific data at signal-to-noise ratios as low as $0.001$, where it generates plausible particle images from severely corrupted measurements.

</details>


### [3] [PhysAgent: A Multi-Agent Framework for Reliable Remote Heart Rate Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00066)
*Yehui Yang,Bo Zhao,Junzhe Cao,Hui Ma,Yue Sun,Wenjin Wang,Zitong Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysAgent 是一个基于多智能体候选验证框架的远程光电容积描记（rPPG）心率估计方法，利用轻量级 4B MLLM（Qwen3-VL-4B）对多个基础估计器的输出进行推理验证与融合，提升心率估计的稳定性和可靠性。

Research relevance: 与本研究方向弱相关（2分）。该论文属于泛视觉/生理信号估计领域（remote photoplethysmography, rPPG），不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱或多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测任务。虽然论文中提到了"lightweight 4B MLLM"，但这里的"轻量"是指模型参数量相对较小（4B），并非面向边缘/星上部署的模型压缩或加速方法，与轻量化（pruning/quantization/distillation/NAS）无关。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，属于生理信号估计领域，且依赖 4B 规模 MLLM，与星上部署需求严重不符。仅"多候选验证"和"确定性融合"的设计思想有极有限的间接借鉴价值，不建议优先精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：远程光电容积描记（rPPG）心率估计，属于生理信号估计/回归任务。应用场景：从人脸视频中非接触式估计心率。传感器类型：普通RGB摄像头（人脸视频）。目标类别：不涉及目标检测，目标为心率数值预测。摘要未说明具体数据集细节，仅提及"multiple public rPPG benchmarks"。

Model architecture: PhysAgent 框架由三部分组成：(1) 多个基础 rPPG 估计器（base estimators）作为候选生成器，各自独立输出心率或脉搏波形的候选结果；(2) 一个轻量级 4B 多模态大语言模型 Qwen3-VL-4B 驱动多智能体推理，对视频条件、信号可靠性和候选分歧进行推理验证；(3) 一个确定性的生理验证器（deterministic physiological verifier）检查融合提案，并通过可复现的数值融合过程输出最终心率。该方法不训练新的基础 rPPG 模型，也不让 MLLM 直接输出心率。

Lightweight method: 论文中"lightweight"仅指使用参数量为 4B 的 Qwen3-VL-4B 作为推理引擎，相对于更大的 MLLM 而言较轻量。但这不是面向边缘/星上部署的模型压缩方法，不涉及剪枝（pruning）、量化（quantization）、知识蒸馏（knowledge distillation）、NAS 或轻量网络设计。摘要未说明任何部署相关的轻量化技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文未提供参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。值得注意的是，即便 Qwen3-VL-4B 被称为"lightweight"，4B 参数规模的 MLLM 在星上/边缘设备上部署仍极具挑战，与在轨遥感目标检测的实时推理需求差距很大。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要仅提及"multiple public rPPG benchmarks"，未列出具体数据集名称。评价指标方面，摘要提到"fusion stability and reliability"，但未给出具体指标名称（如 MAE, RMSE, Pearson correlation 等）。摘要未说明实验设置的细节。

Experiments: 摘要提到在多个公开 rPPG 基准上，PhysAgent 相比直接 MLLM 预测和不受约束的集成融合方法，提升了融合稳定性和可靠性，并避免了不可复现性和生理不一致性问题。但摘要未给出具体数值结果、对比方法列表或消融实验细节。

Limitations: (1) 与遥感/卫星/目标检测完全无关，属于生理信号估计领域；(2) 依赖 4B 规模 MLLM 进行推理，计算开销大，不具备边缘/星上部署可行性；(3) 摘要未说明推理延迟、能耗等关键部署指标；(4) 多智能体推理框架的推理时间可能远高于直接预测方法；(5) 摘要未说明在资源受限场景下的适用性。

Ideas for my research: 尽管该论文与遥感目标检测无关，但以下思路可借鉴：(1) "多候选验证"思想——在遥感小目标检测中，多个检测器可能对同一目标给出冲突但各自合理的候选框，可借鉴多智能体验证框架对候选框进行交叉验证与融合，提升检测可靠性；(2) "确定性验证器 + 可复现融合"的设计理念——在星上推理中，可设计确定性的后处理验证模块来约束模型输出，避免不可复现性；(3) 多源信息推理——借鉴其利用视频条件、信号可靠性等多维度信息进行推理的思路，在遥感场景中可融合多光谱/多时相信息进行目标验证。但需注意，其依赖大语言模型的推理范式与星上轻量化部署需求相悖，不宜直接迁移。

Abstract: Remote photoplethysmography (rPPG) enables non-contact heart-rate estimation from facial videos, but its weak physiological signal is easily corrupted by motion, illumination changes, occlusion, skin-appearance variation, and device noise. Existing rPPG methods typically rely on a single model to directly predict heart rate or recover pulse waveforms, while different strong estimators may produce conflicting yet individually plausible candidates for the same video. To resolve these conflicts, we propose PhysAgent, an inference-time multi-agent candidate-verification framework. Unlike direct prediction approaches, PhysAgent neither trains a new base rPPG model nor asks Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to output heart rate directly. In contrast, it treats outputs from multiple base estimators as physiological hypotheses to be verified and uses a lightweight 4B MLLM, Qwen3-VL-4B, to drive multi-agent reasoning over video conditions, signal reliability, and candidate disagreement. A deterministic physiological verifier checks the fusion proposal, and a reproducible numerical fusion process produces the final heart rate. Experimental results on multiple public rPPG benchmarks show that PhysAgent improves fusion stability and reliability across different datasets and source-domain settings, while avoiding the irreproducibility and physiological inconsistency of direct MLLM prediction or unconstrained ensemble fusion. The code will be released soon.

</details>


### [4] [SafeBuild-Bench: A Temporal-Robust Construction Safety Benchmark with Graph-Enhanced Data Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00068)
*Yi Cui,Zilin Wang,Yijie Xu,Qianyi Cai,Huizai Yao,Shuai Jiang,Bingzhuo Zhong,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SafeBuild-Bench，一个面向建筑施工安全场景的多模态大语言模型（MLLM）基准测试，并开发GEMS图增强多模态选择流水线用于从冗余工业数据中筛选高信息量样本。

Research relevance: 与本研究方向弱相关（2分）。论文聚焦建筑施工安全场景的多模态大语言模型评估与数据筛选，属于泛视觉/泛机器学习方法，不涉及遥感、卫星、在轨部署、目标检测或模型轻量化。虽然涉及多模态模型和benchmark构建，但场景、任务、传感器类型均与遥感在轨目标检测无关。

Reading priority: 1分。论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，属于建筑施工安全场景的MLLM评估工作。仅数据筛选思路和benchmark设计有极有限的间接借鉴价值，不建议优先精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：建筑施工安全场景下的多模态大语言模型评估（multiple-choice hazard identification 和 free-form hazard description）；应用场景：建筑工地安全巡检；传感器类型：工业图像-文本记录（非遥感传感器）；目标类别：施工安全隐患（如工人靠近无护栏脚手架边缘）。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体MLLM架构。核心方法为GEMS（graph-enhanced multimodal selection pipeline），结合proxy-model confusion signal与graph-based diversity来筛选信息量高的候选样本。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等模型压缩或加速方法。GEMS属于数据选择/筛选方法，用于降低标注成本，而非模型轻量化。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。论文关注的是模型评估与数据筛选，不涉及边缘或星上部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：SafeBuild-Bench，从100K+工业图像-文本记录中挖掘，包含3,314个任务实例，来自3,000+张专家验证图像；另有公开instruction-tuning数据用于GEMS子集选择评估。评价指标：准确率（best overall score near 60），具体指标名称摘要未说明。

Experiments: 实验包括：1) 在公开instruction-tuning数据上验证GEMS选择的子集在较小数据预算下保持robustness-oriented性能；2) 在SafeBuild-Bench上评估当前MLLMs，最佳综合得分约60，说明现有MLLM在施工安全理解上仍不可靠。

Limitations: 与本研究方向基本无关，缺乏遥感、目标检测、轻量化、部署相关任何信息。摘要未说明具体MLLM架构、推理成本、数据长尾分布的处理细节，也未提供与遥感场景的任何关联。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限，但有两处可参考：1) GEMS中"proxy-model confusion signal + graph-based diversity"的数据筛选思路，可迁移到遥感小样本/长尾目标检测训练数据的高效筛选，降低标注成本；2) 构建benchmark时考虑"时间与场地变化"（temporal and site variation）的元数据驱动设计，对遥感多时相、多场景目标检测评测有参考价值。

Abstract: Construction-safety models must handle concrete deployment risks, such as a worker standing near a scaffold edge without guardrails, rather than only recognize common objects in curated images. Yet real inspection archives are redundant, long-tailed, and collected across changing sites and months. We introduce SafeBuild-Bench, a metadata-driven benchmark for evaluating multimodal large language models on construction safety under realistic temporal and site variation. It is mined from 100K+ industrial image-text records and contains 3,314 task instances from over 3,000 expert-verified images, covering multiple-choice hazard identification and free-form hazard description. To make expert verification scalable, we develop GEMS, a graph-enhanced multimodal selection pipeline that combines a proxy-model confusion signal with graph-based diversity to identify informative candidates from redundant streams. On public instruction-tuning data, GEMS-selected subsets preserve robustness-oriented performance under small data budgets. On SafeBuild-Bench, current MLLMs remain far from reliable construction-safety understanding, with the best overall score near 60. We release the benchmark, evaluation scripts, and GEMS codebase at https://github.com/safebuild/gems.

</details>


### [5] [Empirical investigation of 3D CT Foundation Models and Unsupervised Adaptation for Head and Neck Cancer Recurrence Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00071)
*Bilel Guetarni,Feryal Windal,David Pasquier,Halim Benhabiles*

Main category: cs.CV

Reading priority: 1分。论文属于医学影像预后预测方向，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化基本无关，不建议投入阅读时间。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关借鉴点有限，但可抽象出两点方法论启示：(1) foundation model在跨分布场景下的泛化评估框架（external validation）思路，可类比遥感模型在不同传感器/不同地域影像上的泛化测试；(2) 模态融合（影像+临床数据）优于单一模态的结论，可类比遥感中光学+SAR多模态融合对检测性能的提升。但均非直接可复现的轻量化或检测技术。

Abstract: The rapid emergence of 3D CT foundation models has opened new avenues for predictive modeling from CT imaging, offering a compelling alternative to traditional radiomics which is known to suffer from reproducibility issues and sensitivity to acquisition protocol variations. Yet, as these models grow in availability, a critical need arises to evaluate how well their learned representations generalize across diverse clinical settings and whether adaptation to specific downstream tasks is necessary to unlock their full potential. To address these questions, we benchmarked several 3D CT foundation models for predicting recurrence-free survival in head and neck cancer across two public datasets totaling 3,644 patients, evaluating various adaptation strategies and modality fusion mechanisms. Our findings reveal persistent difficulty in identifying features that generalize consistently across different imaging distributions, as evidenced by significant performance drops on external validation cohorts. Ultimately, the integration of imaging features with clinical data remains the most accurate approach for prognostic prediction, though achieving universal generalization across varied clinical contexts continues to represent a substantial challenge for the current generation of models.

Limitations: 1. 任务为图像分类而非目标检测，与在轨目标检测方向不直接相关；2. 完全不涉及模型轻量化/压缩/加速技术；3. 量子计算硬件在星上部署可行性极低，与在轨实时推理需求脱节；4. 摘要未提供任何量化性能指标（accuracy、参数量、FLOPs、延迟等）；5. 火山云与气象云相似性高、喷发特征多变、静止轨道传感器光谱采样粗，这些是任务本身的难点，但摘要未说明如何针对性解决。

Abstract: Recent advances in quantum computing are opening new possibilities for Earth Observation (EO) data analysis. Quantum machine learning (QML) approaches offer novel ways to process information by exploiting quantum phenomena such as superposition and entanglement. These capabilities have motivated the exploration of whether quantum-enhanced models can address long-standing challenges in satellite remote sensing, where complex spectral and spatial signals often require sophisticated feature extraction. Among various fields of application, EO data allow the global monitoring of volcanic clouds and are crucial for aviation safety, hazard assessment, real-time eruption response, and evaluation of volcanic impacts on climate. Yet accurate detection of volcanic clouds remains difficult due to their similarity with meteorological clouds, the variability of eruption signatures, and the coarse spectral sampling of geostationary sensors. In this work, the potential of hybrid quantum convolutional neural networks (QCNNs) for the classification of satellite images containing volcanic clouds was investigated. These architectures integrate quantum computational layers into a classical convolutional framework. Two QCNN variants (with 2 and 4 qubits) have been considered to evaluate their ability to classify a dataset of SEVIRI images, including scenes with volcanic clouds (composed of ash, $SO_2$, or mixed components) as well as non-volcanic backgrounds. Finally, the performance of the hybrid QCNN models was compared with that of purely classical architectures.

</details>

Task and scene: 任务类型：纵向医学影像数据集划分与队列平衡（dataset partitioning / cohort balancing）。应用场景：临床纵向影像工作流（longitudinal clinical imaging）。传感器类型：contrast-enhanced T1-weighted brain MRI。目标类别：不涉及目标检测，无目标类别。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体深度学习模型架构。核心方法为：Tripartite Dataset Analytics Framework（空间网格完整性、多参数强度指纹、纵向时间轨迹三方面表征）+ elbow-optimized K-means clustering（六维联合强度-时间特征空间）+ intra-cluster proportionate stratified sampling。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：纵向contrast-enhanced T1-weighted brain MRI队列（N=149）。评价指标：跨子集强度偏差（cross-subset intensity bias，从随机划分的34.1%降至2.1%以下）、纵向随访间隔对齐程度（aligned around population mean）、Monte Carlo压力测试（10个随机种子×3种划分配置）。

Experiments: 在N=149的纵向脑MRI队列上验证：最大跨子集强度偏差从随机洗牌下的34.1%降至2.1%以下；纵向随访间隔与总体均值紧密对齐；Monte Carlo压力测试（10 seeds × 3 split configs）确认对齐结果紧密有界，与随机划分的显著变异性形成对比。

Ideas for my research: 1. 借鉴其"数据集划分质量影响模型评估可靠性"的思想：在遥感目标检测中，若训练/验证/测试集划分不当（如同一区域不同时相图像泄漏），会导致在轨部署时性能高估。可考虑对遥感数据集做时空划分审计。2. 其"六维联合特征空间聚类+分层抽样"的队列平衡思路，可迁移到遥感多时相/多传感器数据（如SAR+光学）的域平衡划分，减少域偏移对轻量化模型评估的影响。3. 但整体而言，该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的直接可借鉴点较少，仅作为数据工程方法论参考。

Abstract: Rigorous dataset partitioning is a foundational, yet frequently overlooked, prerequisite for reliable deep learning in longitudinal medical imaging. Naively shuffling small clinical cohorts routinely introduces covariate shifts and temporal sampling imbalances across training, validation, and test subsets, exposing downstream models to out-of-distribution evaluation. We address this vulnerability with an auditable Tripartite Dataset Analytics Framework that systematically characterizes spatial grid integrity, multi-parametric intensity fingerprints, and longitudinal temporal trajectories, quantifying the heavy-tailed feature dispersion and irregular, episodic sampling intervals typical of real-world clinical cohorts. Building on this characterization, we formalize an unsupervised spatio-temporal cohort-balancing standard operating procedure (SOP) that combines elbow-optimized K-means clustering over a standardized, six-dimensional joint intensity-temporal feature space with intra-cluster proportionate stratified sampling. On a longitudinal, contrast-enhanced $T1$-weighted brain MRI cohort (N=149), the protocol reduces the maximum cross-subset intensity bias from 34.1% under conventional random shuffling to under 2.1%, while aligning longitudinal follow-up intervals closely around the population mean. Monte Carlo stress testing across ten random seeds and three split configurations confirms that this alignment remains tightly bounded, in clear contrast to the substantial variability of random partitioning. The resulting protocol offers a reproducible, generalizable procedure for cohort engineering in variable-length longitudinal clinical imaging workflows.

</details>

*Nevio Dubbini,Daniel P. van Helden,Claudia Sciuto,Martina Naso,Arthur Leck,Clement Joubert,Heeli C. Schechter,Remy Chapoulie,Gabriele Gattiglia*

TL;DR: 提出一个面向考古数字化工作流的多模态机器学习框架，用于标定监测、质量评估和自适应采集支持，覆盖摄影测量3D重建、高光谱成像、X射线荧光光谱和拉曼光谱等多种传感模态。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Model architecture: 统一pipeline架构，结合：确定性质量指标（deterministic quality indicators）、统计特征表示（statistical feature representations）、机器学习分类（machine-learning classification）、异常检测（anomaly detection）、可解释人工智能（explainable AI, XAI）。每种传感模态通过结构化特征空间表示，编码几何、光谱、空间和统计属性。采用监督学习（supervised learning）与无监督学习（unsupervised learning）相结合的方法。

Ideas for my research: 1. 多模态质量评估思路可借鉴：在轨遥感目标检测中，可借鉴其"确定性指标+统计特征+ML分类+异常检测"的pipeline，用于检测卫星影像采集质量（如云遮挡、传感器退化、信噪比下降），作为检测模型的前置质量筛选模块；2. XAI（可解释AI）方法可用于分析在轨检测模型的误检原因，辅助轻量化剪枝时判断哪些通道/特征对检测贡献更大；3. 高光谱传感模态的质量退化模式分析（如spectral distortions, baseline fluctuations, low signal-to-noise）对高光谱遥感目标检测的数据预处理有参考价值；4. "resource-aware acquisition strategies"思想可类比为在轨资源受限条件下的自适应采集调度策略。

Abstract: This paper presents a multimodal machine-learning framework for calibration monitoring, quality assessment, and adaptive acquisition support in archaeological digitisation workflows. The proposed approach operates across photogrammetric 3D reconstruction, hyperspectral imaging, X-ray fluorescence spectroscopy, and Raman spectroscopy through a unified pipeline combining deterministic quality indicators, statistical feature representations, machine-learning classification, anomaly detection, and explainable artificial intelligence (XAI). Rather than replacing instrument-level calibration, the framework introduces an additional algorithmic layer that evaluates whether acquisitions are statistically consistent, physically plausible, and suitable for downstream multimodal integration. For each sensing modality, acquisitions are represented through structured feature spaces encoding geometric, spectral, spatial, and statistical properties. These representations are used to identify degradation patterns such as reconstruction artefacts, illumination inconsistencies, spectral distortions, detector instability, baseline fluctuations, and low signal-to-noise conditions. Supervised and unsupervised learning methods are combined with XAI techniques to support both automatic discrimination between acceptable and problematic acquisitions and interpretation of the underlying causes of degradation. The framework additionally supports adaptive feedback and resource-aware acquisition strategies by linking feature-space deviations to acquisition-level corrective actions. Experimental results obtained on multimodal archaeological datasets demonstrate that the proposed methodology captures meaningful acquisition variability and enables robust quality assessment across heterogeneous sensing modalities.

</details>


### [9] [K-space Gaussian Representation for Parallel MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00075)
*Yu Guan,Mingyu Hu,Jiale Hu,Zhuoxu Cui,Dong Liang,Qiegen Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出K-space Gaussian Representation (KGR)，在原生k-space域用Gabor-Gaussian基元显式参数化连续信号，并通过低秩流形投影和频率自适应拟合实现加速MRI重建。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2分）。该论文属于医学影像（MRI）重建领域，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景完全无关；不涉及object detection；也不涉及pruning、quantization、knowledge distillation、NAS等轻量化方法。仅其"连续信号显式参数化+低秩约束"的数学建模思路可能对遥感图像重建/超分有间接借鉴意义，但整体与本研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文属于医学MRI重建领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不涉及目标检测、遥感场景或任何部署/压缩方法，仅数学建模思路有极有限的间接借鉴意义，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：加速磁共振成像（Accelerated MRI）的k-space信号重建。应用场景：医学影像（MRI），非遥感/卫星场景。传感器类型：MRI多线圈采集（multi-coil）。目标类别：不涉及目标检测，无目标类别。

Model architecture: KGR（K-space Gaussian Representation）：在原生k-space域用Gabor-Gaussian基元（Gabor-Gaussian primitives）显式参数化连续信号，基元共享空间几何（shared spatial geometry）以保持线圈间相关性；估计的表示被投影到低秩流形（low-rank manifold）上，以施加由平滑相位和线圈冗余带来的代数约束；采用频率自适应拟合策略（frequency-adaptive fitting strategy）适应不同k-space区域的异质特性。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等模型压缩或加速方法。其"紧凑表示（compact representation）"和"低秩投影"属于信号建模层面的参数效率优化，而非面向部署的模型轻量化。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未提供参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。且该工作面向医学MRI重建，与星上/边缘部署场景无关。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称。提到"multiple datasets and sampling schemes"（多个数据集和采样方案），评价指标为定量指标（quantitative metrics）和视觉质量（visual quality），但未给出具体指标名称（如PSNR、SSIM等）。

Experiments: 摘要未说明具体对比方法名称。仅提到"consistent improvements over representative reconstruction baselines"（相对代表性重建基线的一致改进），在定量指标和视觉质量上均有提升。未给出消融实验细节。

Limitations: 1. 面向医学MRI，与遥感/卫星/目标检测完全无关；2. 摘要未提供任何部署相关指标（参数量、FLOPs、延迟等）；3. 未说明推理速度与实时性；4. 未给出具体数据集和基线方法名称，可复现性信息不足；5. 连续参数化表示的计算开销和训练复杂度未说明。

Ideas for my research: 1. 尽管场景不同，其"显式连续参数化+低秩流形投影"的建模思想可借鉴到遥感图像重建/超分辨率任务中，例如对遥感影像的连续域建模；2. "紧凑表示"和"低秩约束"的思路可用于遥感目标检测骨干网络的特征压缩，减少冗余通道；3. 频率自适应拟合策略可类比遥感多光谱/高光谱不同波段异质特性的自适应处理；4. 但整体借鉴价值有限，不建议作为核心参考。

Abstract: Accelerated magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) aims to recover the k-space signal from acquired measurements, where accurate estimation of missing samples is essential for high-fidelity reconstruction. Existing k-space reconstruction methods estimate missing samples through interpolation operators or structure priors defined on discrete sampling grids. Although these formulations effectively exploit local interpolation relationships and global k-space redundancy, they reconstruct only discrete frequency coefficients and therefore do not explicitly model the underlying continuous signal. To overcome this limitation, we propose K-space Gaussian Representation (KGR), the first explicit continuous representation formulated directly in the native k-space domain. Rather than estimating unknown samples on discrete grids, KGR parameterizes the continuous signal using Gabor-Gaussian primitives with shared spatial geometry, yielding a compact representation that naturally preserves inter-coil correlations. Because unconstrained continuous fitting does not necessarily satisfy the intrinsic structural properties of multi-coil signal, the estimated representation is projected onto a low-rank manifold to enforce the algebraic constraints arising from smoothly varying phase and coil redundancy. A frequency-adaptive fitting strategy accommodates the heterogeneous characteristics of different k-space regions. Comprehensive validation across multiple datasets and sampling schemes shows consistent improvements over representative reconstruction baselines in both quantitative metrics and visual quality. These results suggest that explicit continuous parameterization of native k-space provides a principled framework for integrating continuous signal modeling with structured low-rank reconstruction.

</details>


### [10] [Which Modality Decides? Counterfactual Modality Attribution for Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00076)
*Vahidin Hasic,Chao Wang,Luis C. Garcia-Peraza-Herrera,David Watson,Senka Krivic*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Counterfactual Modality Attribution (CMA)框架，用反事实生成和Shapley值博弈论量化多模态大语言模型（MLLM）中各模态对预测的贡献，用于可解释性审计。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2分）。论文聚焦多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的可解释性/模态归因，属于泛视觉-语言基础模型方向，与遥感、卫星、在轨目标检测、轻量化部署均无直接关联。既非遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测，更不涉及任何模型压缩或加速方法。仅有的间接关联在于：MLLM可能被用于遥感图像理解，且可解释性审计思想对安全关键系统（如星上推理）有潜在借鉴价值，但论文本身未涉及遥感或部署。

Reading priority: 1分。论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，属于多模态大模型可解释性研究。仅对可解释性审计思想有极有限的间接参考价值，不建议优先精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的模态级可解释性/归因分析（modality attribution）。应用场景：安全关键决策系统（safety-critical applications），包括受控合成基准和真实多模态临床数据集。传感器类型：图像+文本（多模态），非遥感传感器。目标类别：不涉及具体目标检测类别。

Model architecture: 提出Counterfactual Modality Attribution (CMA)框架，包含：(1) 基于耦合扩散先验（coupled diffusion priors）生成image-only、text-only和joint multimodal反事实样本；(2) 基于Shapley值的合作博弈论公式将反事实转化为模态归因分数。框架本身是解释性方法，不涉及具体检测网络结构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等任何模型压缩或加速方法，也不涉及实时推理或边缘/星上部署。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署相关信息。且CMA基于扩散模型和Shapley值计算，计算开销较大，与在轨轻量化部署方向相悖。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：(1) 受控合成基准（controlled synthetic benchmarks），具有已知ground-truth模态依赖；(2) 真实世界多模态临床数据集（real-world multimodal clinical dataset）。评价指标：模态归因正确率（在98%受控案例中正确识别决策驱动模态），以及与baseline方法的对比表现。

Experiments: 主要结果：CMA在98%的受控案例中正确识别决策驱动模态，持续优于baseline方法，并揭示了仅靠预测准确率无法发现的跨模态推理失败。摘要未提供与具体检测/分类模型的对比实验或消融实验细节。

Limitations: (1) 与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化完全无关；(2) 基于扩散模型和Shapley值的计算框架计算成本高，不适合在轨实时部署；(3) 仅评估了临床和合成数据，未在遥感/卫星场景验证；(4) 摘要未说明方法在真实遥感多模态数据上的泛化能力；(5) 未报告任何部署相关指标。

Ideas for my research: (1) 模态归因思想可迁移到遥感多模态（如光学+SAR）目标检测中，用于审计模型是否真正依赖关键模态而非捷径学习，这对星上安全决策有参考价值；(2) Shapley值归因框架可启发遥感检测模型的可解释性分析，但需替换为轻量化的归因方法以适配在轨约束；(3) 论文揭示"正确输出但错误证据"的shortcut learning问题，提醒在遥感检测模型轻量化过程中需防止压缩后模型依赖错误的特征线索。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) increasingly support high-stakes decision making by combining complementary information from images and text. While existing explainability methods identify influential image regions or text tokens, they cannot answer a fundamental question: which modality drives a prediction? Consequently, a model may produce the correct output while relying on the wrong source of evidence, masking shortcut learning and unsafe reasoning. We formulate modality attribution as a complementary explainability objective for multimodal foundation models and propose Counterfactual Modality Attribution (CMA), the first framework for quantifying modality-level contributions in MLLMs. CMA generates image-only, text-only, and joint multimodal counterfactuals using coupled diffusion priors and converts them into principled modality attribution scores through a cooperative game-theoretic formulation based on Shapley values. We evaluate CMA on controlled synthetic benchmarks with known ground-truth modality reliance and on a real-world multimodal clinical dataset. CMA correctly identifies the decision-driving modality in 98% of controlled cases and consistently outperforms baselines, revealing failures of cross-modal reasoning that remain invisible to predictive accuracy alone. Our results establish modality attribution as a complementary dimension of explainability beyond feature attribution, providing a principled framework for auditing multimodal foundation models in safety-critical applications.

</details>


### [11] [Beyond Accuracy: Auditing Spatial Provenance in Visual Token Pruning for OCR-Critical MLLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00077)
*Feixiang Liu,Qiang Qiu,Hao Zhang,Xinyue Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向多模态大语言模型(MLLM)视觉token剪枝的证据风险审计框架，将答案质量与token空间来源(provenance)、干预和实际成本耦合，揭示仅凭准确率无法暴露的"答案正确但支撑token已丢失"的失败模式。

Research relevance: 弱相关(2分)。论文属于泛视觉/泛机器学习方法，聚焦多模态大语言模型(MLLM)的visual-token pruning，并非遥感/卫星场景。虽然涉及token剪枝(pruning)这一轻量化技术，但对象是MLLM而非遥感目标检测模型，且不涉及遥感图像、SAR、高光谱等传感器数据，也不涉及在轨部署。其"剪枝需同时报告空间来源与真实成本"的思想对轻量化评估有方法论借鉴意义，但整体与遥感卫星在轨目标检测方向弱相关。

Reading priority: 2分。论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向弱相关，核心对象是MLLM视觉token剪枝评估，不涉及遥感场景。但其"剪枝评估需同时报告空间溯源与真实成本"的方法论思想对轻量化评估协议设计有一定借鉴价值，可作为方法论参考而非直接技术来源。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：多模态大语言模型(MLLM)的视觉token剪枝质量评估与审计。应用场景：text-rich图像理解(OCR密集场景)，如TextVQA、DocVQA。传感器类型：非遥感，为通用自然图像/文档图像。目标类别：OCR文本区域、视觉token。不涉及遥感目标检测。

Model architecture: 研究对象为MLLM，包括Qwen3-VL-8B、LLaVA-1.5-7B、InternVL3.5-8B。方法核心是"evidence-risk audit"框架，耦合答案行为(answer behavior)、几何token来源溯源(geometric token-origin provenance)、干预(interventions)和实际成本(realized cost)；采用transparent training-free selectors(Target/Random/Grid)隔离受控工作点。摘要未说明具体剪枝网络结构细节。

Lightweight method: 涉及visual-token pruning(视觉token剪枝)，属于token级稀疏化/剪枝方法。核心贡献不是提出新的剪枝算法，而是提出一种评估剪枝质量的审计协议：在固定保留预算下，不仅看答案质量，还检查保留token是否在空间上可溯源到支撑答案的OCR区域(positive-support coverage)。同时报告materialized prefixes带来的batch-prefill加速(最高4.32x)和增量峰值内存降低(76.4%)。摘要未说明量化、蒸馏、NAS等其他轻量化手段。

Onboard deployability: 部分相关。摘要报告了materialized prefixes带来最高4.32x batch-prefill加速和76.4%的增量峰值内存(incremental peak memory)降低，涉及延迟和内存指标。但对象是8B量级的MLLM，参数量大(7B-8B)，与星上/边缘部署的轻量级遥感目标检测模型(通常为MB级)差距巨大。未报告FLOPs、能耗、具体硬件平台(FPGA/NPU/TensorRT)等部署信息。摘要未说明。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：TextVQA、DocVQA(用于full-validation验证)；另有locked image-disjoint confirmation设置。指标：observed accuracy(准确率)、positive-support coverage(正支撑覆盖率，衡量保留token对支撑答案OCR区域的空间可溯源覆盖)、paired image-cluster difference及95% CI、batch-prefill speedup、incremental peak memory。摘要未说明遥感数据集。

Experiments: 关键实验发现：Qwen Target在30% retention下observed accuracy 0.786 vs Full 0.783(差异+0.003，95% CI [-0.014, +0.020])，准确率几乎持平，但同预算下Target/Random/Grid的positive-support coverage差异显著(0.620/0.270/0.318)，说明准确率无法暴露支撑token丢失问题。跨Qwen3-VL-8B、LLaVA-1.5-7B、InternVL3.5-8B验证了model-specific quality-risk-traceability frontiers。TextVQA和DocVQA full-validation显示favorable target-verification points不意味着task-general compression。

Limitations: 1) 与遥感/卫星/在轨部署完全无关，无任何遥感数据或场景。2) 对象是7B-8B量级MLLM，与轻量级遥感目标检测模型(通常<100MB)规模差异巨大。3) 摘要未说明剪枝后模型在真实硬件上的端到端延迟、能耗、吞吐量。4) 未涉及旋转框检测、小目标检测等遥感特有任务。5) 未报告FLOPs、参数量等经典轻量化指标。6) 方法聚焦OCR/text-rich场景，对遥感目标检测的迁移性未验证。

Ideas for my research: 1) 方法论借鉴：其"剪枝评估不能只看准确率，还要检查保留特征/通道的空间来源是否覆盖支撑目标检测的关键区域"的思想，可迁移到遥感小目标检测的剪枝评估中——即剪枝后小目标(如舰船、飞机)对应的特征图区域是否仍有有效通道保留，可设计"positive-support coverage"式的遥感目标级溯源指标。2) 评估协议借鉴：在固定压缩预算下同时报告质量、空间溯源覆盖率和实际成本(延迟/内存)的三维评估框架，可用于遥感轻量化模型对比。3) 其"favorable target-verification points不意味着task-general compression"的结论提醒：在遥感场景中，某一数据集上剪枝效果好不代表跨场景(如SAR vs 光学)通用，需多数据集验证。4) training-free selector的设计思路可用于遥感轻量化中的通道/token选择策略。

Abstract: Visual-token pruning is usually judged by answer quality at a fixed retention budget. For text-rich multimodal large language models (MLLMs), this protocol can miss a distinct failure: an answer remains correct even when no retained token is locally traceable to the small OCR region that supports it. We turn this blind spot into an evidence-risk audit that couples answer behavior with geometric token-origin provenance, interventions, and realized cost; transparent training-free selectors isolate controlled operating points. On locked image-disjoint confirmation, Qwen Target at 30% retention has observed accuracy 0.786 versus 0.783 for Full (paired image-cluster difference +0.003, 95% CI [-0.014, +0.020]), yet same-budget Target, Random, and Grid retain sharply different positive-support coverage: 0.620, 0.270, and 0.318. Across Qwen3-VL-8B, LLaVA-1.5-7B, and InternVL3.5-8B, matched controls, interventions, detector tests, and external methods reveal model-specific quality-risk-traceability frontiers that accuracy alone does not expose. Materialized prefixes yield up to 4.32x batch-prefill speedup and 76.4% lower incremental peak memory; full-validation TextVQA and DocVQA further show that favorable target-verification points do not imply task-general compression. Visual-token pruning should therefore report surviving spatial provenance and realized cost alongside quality and compression.

</details>


### [12] [Device-First Feedback: Toward Mobile-Native LLM-Driven Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00078)
*Saif U Din,Muhammad Ahsan Hussain,Radu Timofte,Dmitry Ignatov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个自动化移动端部署流水线，将LLM生成的CNN架构通过QLoRA微调、GPU评估、INT8导出和真机基准测试闭环迭代，但实验表明GPU精度提升并不保证移动端性能单调增长。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2分）。该论文涉及CNN模型在移动硬件上的部署（INT8量化、TensorFlow Lite导出、延迟测量），与"轻量化/边缘部署"主题有方法学上的借鉴意义，但完全不涉及遥感、卫星、在轨目标检测等场景，属于泛视觉/泛机器学习方法。

Reading priority: 2分。论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测无直接关联，但其"GPU精度与部署端性能不一致"的结论和闭环部署流水线设计对轻量化部署方法论有一定借鉴价值，可作为补充阅读而非核心参考。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：移动端CNN模型自动部署流水线；应用场景：通用移动设备（Samsung平板）；传感器类型：无（通用图像分类）；目标类别：CIFAR-10/CIFAR-100通用图像分类，非遥感目标。

Model architecture: 由LLM生成CNN架构（architecture-generating LLM），通过QLoRA微调架构生成器；模型需支持INT8 TensorFlow Lite导出。摘要未说明具体CNN结构细节。

Lightweight method: INT8量化（TensorFlow Lite导出）、delegate selection（硬件加速委托选择）、on-device latency测量、QLoRA微调架构生成LLM。属于量化+自动化部署流水线，不涉及剪枝、蒸馏或NAS。

Onboard deployability: 部署目标为Samsung SM-P613平板（移动端而非星上）。评估指标包括INT8量化精度（CIFAR-10上均值46.9%）、mobile deployment score（提升约25.6x）、on-device延迟。摘要未说明具体参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗数据。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100；指标：GPU validation accuracy、INT8 quantized accuracy、mobile deployment score、on-device latency；实验设置：seed 42，每周期20个模型，cycles 0-6，95%置信区间。

Experiments: CIFAR-10上cycle 1被gate接受，mobile deployment score较基线提升约25.6x，量化精度均值46.9%；后续周期提升GPU精度但未通过非递减mobile gate。CIFAR-100上pre-QLoRA基线保持最佳mobile score，迭代提升GPU精度（最高26.2%）但无法超越cycle 0的真机表现，训练池在首轮接受后停滞于19个样本。结论：闭环GPU微调不保证移动端单调增益。

Limitations: 1) 与遥感/卫星/在轨场景完全无关；2) 仅验证了通用图像分类（CIFAR），未涉及目标检测任务；3) 部署平台为平板而非星载硬件（FPGA/NPU）；4) 摘要未说明模型参数量、FLOPs、能耗等关键部署指标；5) 实验规模较小（每周期20个模型），泛化性存疑。

Ideas for my research: 1) "GPU精度提升≠部署端性能提升"的结论对在轨场景有警示意义——在遥感目标检测轻量化中，不能只看GPU上的mAP，必须结合INT8量化精度和真机延迟做联合评估；2) 闭环自动化流水线（fine-tune→量化→真机benchmark→gate反馈）的设计思路可借鉴到星上部署流程，形成"训练-量化-星上验证"的闭环；3) 非递减gate机制（non-decreasing gate）可作为在轨模型版本更新的准入策略，防止部署后性能回退；4) INT8 TensorFlow Lite导出+delegate selection的工程流程可迁移到星载NPU/FPGA部署。

Abstract: Deploying convolutional neural networks generated by large language models (LLMs) on real mobile hardware requires more than GPU validation accuracy: INT8 TensorFlow Lite export, delegate selection, and on-device latency jointly determine whether a model is usable. We present an automated mobile deployment pipeline that closes the loop from QLoRA fine-tuning of an architecture-generating LLM through GPU evaluation, INT8 export, and physical-device benchmarking to gated augmentation of the training corpus. The pipeline is fully scripted and runs cycle-by-cycle without manual intervention, with resume support after interruptions. We evaluate the same frozen protocol on two benchmarks, CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100, on a Samsung SM-P613 tablet (seed 42, 20 models per cycle, cycles 0-6). On CIFAR-10, cycle 1 is gate-accepted and improves the mobile deployment score approximately 25.6x over the baseline with a mean quantized accuracy of 46.9%; later cycles raise GPU accuracy but fail the non-decreasing mobile gate. On CIFAR-100, the pre-QLoRA baseline retains the best mobile score; iterative rounds improve GPU accuracy (up to 26.2%) yet cannot surpass cycle 0 on-device, and the training pool stalls at 19 examples after the first accepted round. Together, the two studies show that closed-loop GPU fine-tuning does not guarantee monotonic mobile gains, especially on harder classification tasks, and that multi-dataset, on-device measurement is needed to stress-test deployment objectives. We release per-cycle metrics with 95% confidence intervals, all figures, and complete reproduction commands.

</details>


### [13] [LeapTalk: Breaking the Latency-Quality Trade-off in Talking Head Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00079)
*Rongxiang Zhang,Songhua Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LeapTalk提出一种基于单步bridge蒸馏的实时长视频说话人头部生成框架，通过data-to-data传输、SNR对齐异构蒸馏和音频驱动CFG实现200 FPS的稳定实时生成。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2分）。论文属于泛视觉/生成式AI领域，聚焦talking-head视频生成，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、轻量化部署均无直接关联。唯一可借鉴的是其"单步蒸馏"和"异构蒸馏"思想，在模型压缩/加速方法论层面有一定参考价值，但场景完全不同。

Reading priority: 1分。论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，属于生成式AI/数字人领域。虽然单步蒸馏和异构蒸馏方法论有一定参考价值，但场景差异过大，优先级很低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：长视频实时说话人头部生成（talking-head generation）。应用场景：数字人、视频会议、内容生成。传感器类型：不适用（非遥感）。目标类别：人脸/说话人头部。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: LeapTalk框架包含：1) 基于Brownian bridge的data-to-data传输模型（区别于传统noise-to-data扩散范式）；2) 预训练diffusion teacher到student bridge model的异构蒸馏框架，采用SNR-aligned时间变换Φ(τ)桥接两模型功能差异；3) 音频驱动的classifier-free guidance机制以保持极端步数缩减下的唇形同步。

Lightweight method: 核心轻量化手段是单步bridge蒸馏（single-step bridge distillation），将多步扩散模型蒸馏为单步生成模型，实现从多步推理到1步推理的加速。此外采用异构蒸馏（heterogeneous distillation）实现从diffusion teacher到bridge student的知识迁移。这些属于生成模型加速/蒸馏方法，与目标检测常用的剪枝、量化、NAS等轻量化手段不同。

Onboard deployability: 摘要提到达到200 FPS的生成速度，但未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存占用、能耗等部署指标。且该模型面向视频生成任务，非遥感在轨推理场景，不具备星上部署可行性。摘要未说明具体硬件平台。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称。评价指标方面提及"high-fidelity"、"temporally consistent"、"200 FPS"、"efficiency and stability"，但未给出定量指标（如PSNR、SSIM、FID、LSE-C等）。

Experiments: 摘要仅概述实验结论：单步生成达到200 FPS，在效率和稳定性上显著优于现有方法。未给出具体对比方法名称、消融实验细节和定量结果。

Limitations: 1) 与遥感/卫星/目标检测完全无关，属于生成式AI领域；2) 摘要未提供参数量、FLOPs、硬件平台等部署细节；3) 未说明数据集和定量评价指标；4) 单步蒸馏在生成任务上的成功是否可迁移到判别式检测任务存在不确定性；5) 摘要未讨论模型在边缘/嵌入式设备上的实际部署约束。

Ideas for my research: 尽管场景不同，但以下方法论可借鉴：1) 单步蒸馏思想——若在遥感目标检测中采用teacher-student蒸馏，可探索将多阶段检测器蒸馏为单阶段/单次前向的轻量模型；2) SNR-aligned异构蒸馏——当teacher和student网络结构差异大时（如从大模型蒸馏到轻量backbone），可借鉴这种"对齐"策略来改善知识迁移效果；3) classifier-free guidance的简化思想——在检测任务中可类比为条件引导机制，帮助小目标检测在极端压缩下保持精度。但这些借鉴属于方法论层面的间接启发，需谨慎评估迁移可行性。

Abstract: Long-form and real-time talking-head generation remains challenging due to a latency-quality trade-off: inefficient multi-step diffusion prohibits streaming generation, whereas real-time autoregressive approaches suffer from error accumulation and identity drift. To address this drawback, we propose LeapTalk, a novel framework that achieves stable and real-time talking-head generation with a single forward step, scaling to arbitrarily long videos. At the heart of our approach lies a single-step bridge distillation scheme. On the one hand, departing from the conventional noise-to-data paradigm, we introduce a data-to-data transport formulation based on a Brownian bridge. Anchored by a persistent reference, this strategy effectively mitigates identity drift and enhances long-term temporal stability. On the other hand, to enable smooth knowledge transfer from a pre-trained diffusion teacher to the student bridge model, we explore a heterogeneous distillation framework with an SNR-aligned time transformation $Φ(τ)$, which bridges the functional discrepancy between the two models. Moreover, we propose an audio-driven classifier-free guidance mechanism to maintain fine-grained lip synchronization under extreme step reduction. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves high-fidelity and temporally consistent video generation with only 1 step at up to 200 FPS, significantly outperforming existing approaches in both efficiency and stability. Project Page: https://zhangrongxiang.github.io/leaptalk-page/

</details>


### [14] [Posterior Variance Is a Constraint Map, Not an Error Map: Closed-Form Uncertainty for Radiative Gaussian Splatting in Sparse-View CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13682)
*Chulin Zhao,Yiran Xu,Shu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究Radiative Gaussian splatting在稀疏视角CT重建中的不确定性图（posterior variance）究竟衡量什么，证明其是数据约束图而非误差图，并提出一种闭式解析解以约8倍更低成本精确计算无限样本极限的不确定性，同时通过重参数化修复误差尺度跨场景温度漂移问题。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2分）。本文属于医学CT成像（X-ray rendering, sparse-view CT reconstruction）领域，与遥感/卫星/在轨部署无关，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。虽然涉及Gaussian splatting渲染和不确定性估计，但场景是医学影像重建，不是遥感场景理解或目标检测。其不确定性量化方法（posterior variance、闭式解析解、误差尺度校准）对遥感模型的不确定性估计有一定方法论借鉴意义，但整体与本研究方向弱相关。

Reading priority: 2分。本文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，属于医学CT重建的不确定性量化研究。虽然其不确定性评估方法论和计算加速思想有一定参考价值，但不涉及目标检测、遥感场景或模型压缩，优先级较低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：稀疏视角CT重建中的不确定性量化与误差尺度校准。应用场景：医学影像（clinician读取的组织内部tissue）。传感器类型：X-ray CT。目标类别：无目标检测，涉及组织/物体内部结构。摘要未说明遥感、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等遥感传感器。

Model architecture: 基于Radiative Gaussian splatting（辐射高斯泼溅）的CT重建框架，为每个Gaussian附加后验分布（per-Gaussian posteriors）以生成逐体素不确定性图。利用X-ray渲染在每Gaussian密度上的严格线性性，推导出clamp-aware闭式解，使未修改的rasterizer在前向传播中精确计算不确定性（体积空间和投影空间）。摘要未说明具体网络结构细节。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等传统模型压缩方法。但提出了一种计算效率优化：通过闭式解析解替代采样估计器，将不确定性计算成本降低约8倍（~8x lower cost），这属于算法层面的计算加速而非模型轻量化。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟等部署指标。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、延迟、能耗等部署相关信息。虽然提出约8倍的计算成本降低，但这是相对采样估计器的相对加速，且场景为医学CT而非星上遥感，不具备在轨部署可行性分析。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：官方15场景基准（official 15-scene benchmark）。评价指标：Spearman秩相关系数（median Spearman 0.11）、场景级排名正确率（14/15 scenes）、leave-one-scene-out交叉验证（10/15）、温度跨场景扩散比（19.3x→2.6x）、Poisson预测的-1/2幂律跟踪。摘要未说明具体数据集名称。

Experiments: 主要实验发现：(1) posterior variance在15个场景中14个能正确排名真实误差，但限制在物体内部（临床医生读取的组织）时排名崩溃（median Spearman 0.11，0/15通过）；(2) 三种构造（deep ensemble、严格正log-normal后验、闭式解）和两种估计器家族均无法解决；(3) 约90%的物体内部误差是跨重训练复现的偏差，对模型分歧不可见；(4) 73-81%的全体积相关性由物体/周围对比度承载；(5) 通过重参数化后验将跨场景温度扩散从19.3x压缩到2.6x，单一场景无关温度可迁移到未见场景（10/15 leave-one-scene-out）。

Limitations: 摘要未说明：模型参数量、FLOPs、推理延迟等部署指标；具体网络架构细节；与遥感/卫星场景的任何关联。核心局限是posterior variance在物体内部（实际临床关注区域）无法可靠排名误差，这是方法论层面的根本性缺陷。此外，本文场景为医学CT，与遥感目标检测场景差异显著。

Ideas for my research: 尽管场景不同，本文对不确定性量化的批判性分析对遥感在轨目标检测有方法论启示：(1) 不确定性图（如posterior variance）可能只是数据约束图而非真实误差图，遥感模型的不确定性估计需谨慎验证其是否真正反映检测误差；(2) 闭式解析解替代采样估计器以降低计算成本的思想，可类比用于遥感模型的推理加速；(3) 误差尺度校准（temperature reparameterization）和跨场景泛化验证方法（leave-one-scene-out）可借鉴到遥感多场景目标检测的鲁棒性评估；(4) 偏差分解（bias vs variance）和masked calibration等评估实践可用于诊断遥感检测模型在特定区域（如小目标、密集目标）的误差来源。但整体借鉴价值有限，因为不涉及检测任务和轻量化方法本身。

Abstract: Radiative Gaussian splatting reconstructs sparse-view CT fast and accurately, and recent work attaches per-Gaussian posteriors to yield per-voxel uncertainty maps. We ask what such a map actually measures: posterior variance is a data-constraint map, not an error map -- its alarms are trustworthy, its all-clears are not. Exploiting the strict linearity of X-ray rendering in the per-Gaussian densities, we derive a clamp-aware closed form that the unchanged rasterizer evaluates exactly in one forward pass, in volume and projection space: the infinite-sample limit of the sampling estimator of concurrent work, at ~8x lower cost. On the official 15-scene benchmark this uncertainty ranks true error on 14 of 15 scenes. Restricted to the object interior -- the tissue a clinician reads -- the ranking collapses (median Spearman 0.11, 0/15 pass), identically for a deep ensemble and for a strictly positive log-normal posterior: three constructions, two estimator families, no survivors. The mechanism is structural: about 90% of in-object error is bias that reproduces across retrainings, invisible to model disagreement; 73-81% of the full-volume correlation is carried by object/surround contrast; and an exactly solvable control puts the observed in-object ranking 4-5x below what a perfectly calibrated posterior with the same sigma-spread would score. The error scale, by contrast, is an engineering problem, and we solve it: reparameterizing the posterior contracts the cross-scene temperature spread from 19.3x to 2.6x, one scene-agnostic temperature transfers to unseen scenes (10/15 leave-one-scene-out), and the repaired scale tracks photon count at the Poisson-predicted -1/2 power. We distill evaluation practice that would have caught the illusion -- masked calibration, seed-wise bias decomposition, an exact-posterior reference -- and release all protocols, seeds and per-run evidence.

</details>


### [15] [Beyond Edge Maps: Wavelet-Domain Conditioning for Multi-Adapter Map-to-Satellite Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00083)
*Arisha Prasain*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种仅以独立可获取的制图数据（OSM栅格地图及其平稳小波变换子带）为条件的ControlNet扩散框架，用于从地图合成卫星影像，解决低资源地区卫星底图过时问题。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2-3分）。论文涉及卫星影像（satellite imagery）合成，属于遥感数据生成方向，但核心任务是"地图到卫星影像的生成/合成"（map-to-satellite diffusion），并非目标检测（object detection），也不涉及任何轻量化（lightweight）、剪枝（pruning）、量化（quantization）、知识蒸馏（knowledge distillation）或星上部署（onboard deployment）技术。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题的相关性仅在于其处理的是遥感/卫星影像数据，但任务类型（生成而非检测）和关注点（图像质量而非推理效率）均与本课题方向不同。

Reading priority: 2分。论文与遥感/卫星影像相关，但核心任务是图像生成而非目标检测，且完全不涉及轻量化与部署。对本课题的借鉴价值有限，仅在小波特征用于遥感特征提取和数据增强方面有间接启发。除非对遥感数据生成/增强方向特别感兴趣，否则优先级较低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：地图到卫星影像的生成/合成（map-to-satellite image synthesis），属于条件扩散生成任务。应用场景：低资源地区（如尼泊尔）卫星底图更新与合成。传感器类型：光学卫星影像（satellite imagery）。目标类别：不涉及目标检测，无具体目标类别。数据来源：OpenStreetMap (OSM) 栅格地图 + 平稳小波变换（Stationary Wavelet Transform, SWT）子带。

Model architecture: 基于ControlNet的扩散框架：以冻结的Stable Diffusion为backbone，两个ControlNet适配器分别训练在OSM地图表示和SWT小波子带表示上，通过MultiControlNet融合机制联合利用空间结构与频率细节，无需重新训练多输入模型。条件信号包括OSM栅格地图和其SWT子带（频率域结构信息）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何模型压缩或加速方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等）。虽然使用了冻结的Stable Diffusion backbone（参数冻结可视为一种训练效率策略），但并非面向部署的轻量化设计。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署相关信息。Stable Diffusion + 双ControlNet架构本身计算量巨大，不适合星上/边缘部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：1) 新构建的尼泊尔配对地图-卫星数据集（data-scarce、地形多样区域）；2) Pix2Pix maps-satellite基准数据集。评价指标：SSIM、PSNR、LPIPS（Alex和VGG两种backbone）、FID。实验设置：在8个指标-数据集组合上对比，combined conditioning在6个组合中胜出。

Experiments: 主要结果：combined conditioning（地图+小波）在6/8个指标-数据集对比中胜出——两个数据集上的SSIM和PSNR均最优，自有数据集上的LPIPS（Alex和VGG）最优，在Pix2Pix两个LPIPS backbone上与map-only持平并略优于wavelet-only。Wavelet-only在两个数据集上取得最低FID，体现单图保真度与分布真实感之间的权衡。作者对FID结果持谨慎态度，因测试集规模较小且FID存在小样本偏差。

Limitations: 1) 任务为图像生成而非目标检测，与在轨检测轻量化课题方向不匹配；2) 摘要未报告任何模型效率指标（参数量、FLOPs、延迟等），无法评估部署可行性；3) 测试集规模较小，FID结果存在小样本偏差，作者已承认；4) 未涉及轻量化方法；5) 基于Stable Diffusion的生成框架计算开销大，不适合资源受限的星上环境。

Ideas for my research: 1) 虽然任务不同，但SWT（平稳小波变换）子带作为频率域结构条件信号的思想，可借鉴用于遥感目标检测中的多尺度/多频特征提取，增强小目标检测对高频细节的敏感性；2) 低资源地区（如尼泊尔）数据稀缺场景下的数据增强思路——若在轨检测面临训练数据不足，可考虑利用地图/制图数据合成遥感影像作为训练数据扩充手段；3) MultiControlNet的"冻结backbone + 独立适配器 + 融合"范式，与知识蒸馏中"冻结教师+轻量学生"的思想有类比价值，可启发轻量化检测模型的模块化设计。

Abstract: Commercial mapping partnerships are often unavailable in low-resource regions, leaving satellite basemaps stale and motivating synthesis of satellite imagery from independently maintained cartographic data. Existing ControlNet-based diffusion methods typically condition on structural signals like edges or segmentation extracted from the target image itself, assuming the imagery already exists and limiting their use exactly where synthesis matters most. Map-conditioned alternatives add cues like edge detection but omit frequency-domain structure. We propose a ControlNet-based diffusion framework conditioned only on cartographic sources obtainable independently of the target imagery: OpenStreetMap (OSM) raster maps and their stationary wavelet transform (SWT) subbands, a conditioning signal previously unexplored for map-to-satellite diffusion. Two ControlNet adapters, trained separately on the map and wavelet representations atop a frozen Stable Diffusion backbone, are fused via MultiControlNet, jointly drawing on spatial structure and frequency detail without retraining a multi-input model. We evaluate on a new paired map-satellite dataset curated for Nepal, a data-scarce, topographically diverse region, alongside the Pix2Pix maps-satellite benchmark. Combined conditioning wins six of eight metric-dataset comparisons -- SSIM and PSNR on both datasets, plus LPIPS (both Alex and VGG backbones) on ours and ties map-only on both Pix2Pix LPIPS backbones while still edging past wavelet-only there. Wavelet-only takes the lowest FID on both datasets, matching the tradeoff between per-image fidelity and distributional realism. We treat this gap cautiously given our modest test-set sizes and FID's known small-sample bias.

</details>


### [16] [From Pixels to PCells: A Neurosymbolic Approach to Photonic Component Creation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00084)
*Aadarsh Agarwal,Kenaish Al Qubaisi,Dirk Englund*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PixCell是一个神经符号系统，利用多模态智能体将视觉呈现的光子组件转换为基于几何原语DSL的参数化程序，并通过确定性视觉验证器实现不对称的低成本评估，在光子组件逆向设计任务上显著提升IoU性能。

Research relevance: 弱相关（1-2分）。该论文聚焦于光子组件（photonic component）的视觉到参数化程序转换，属于光子学/EDA设计自动化领域，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能等方向完全无关。虽然涉及多模态智能体、视觉理解、IoU指标和模型训练（LoRA、GRPO），但这些是泛视觉/泛机器学习方法，与遥感目标检测和轻量化部署没有直接关联。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向基本无关，属于光子组件设计自动化领域。除非对多模态智能体+验证器引导的迭代优化范式有跨领域兴趣，否则不建议投入阅读时间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：视觉到参数化程序转换（visual-to-parametric program synthesis），针对光子组件（photonic component）设计。应用场景：光子集成电路设计自动化，涉及interferometer（干涉仪）、SOI/SiN/TFLN波导堆栈。传感器类型：非遥感传感器，为光学显微/视觉图像。目标类别：光子组件（splitter、interferometer等），非遥感目标。

Model architecture: 系统架构为PixCell神经符号系统，包含多模态智能体（multimodal agents）、基于几何原语的小型DSL（domain-specific language）、确定性视觉验证器（deterministic visual verifier）。训练部分使用Qwen3.6-35B-A3B模型，结合LoRA（Low-Rank Adaptation）和GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）进行微调，无需监督演示（without supervised demonstrations）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明传统模型压缩方法（如pruning、quantization、knowledge distillation、NAS）。仅涉及LoRA作为参数高效微调手段，以及Qwen3.6-35B-A3B这一MoE（Mixture-of-Experts）架构模型（A3B表示激活3B参数），这属于模型规模控制范畴，但与遥感目标检测的轻量化部署（FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等）无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文未涉及星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。Qwen3.6-35B-A3B模型规模较大（35B总参数），不适合星上部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：8个光子组件目标（eight component targets）及8个训练排除的论文图（eight training-excluded paper figures）。评价指标：mean best-turn IoU、mean champion IoU、free spectral range（FSR，8.0 nm目标）、footprint约束。实验设置：multi-seed sampling、iterative revision、verifier-guided revision rounds。

Experiments: 主要结果：多模态智能体通过PixCell接口和验证器在8个组件目标上mean IoU超过0.9（最高0.974和0.955），而multi-seed sampling和iterative revision方法mean best-turn IoU仅为0.416。跨堆栈研究在220-nm SOI、400-nm SiN和400-nm TFLN上重建满足8.0 nm FSR目标的程序。Qwen3.6-35B-A3B模型在8个训练排除图上mean champion IoU从0.422提升到0.491（经过3轮验证器引导的修订）。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测方向基本无关。摘要未说明任何遥感场景、目标检测任务、轻量化部署信息。该工作的核心价值在于光子组件设计自动化，属于完全不同的应用领域。即使从泛机器学习角度，其方法（多模态智能体+验证器引导）与遥感目标检测的模型压缩/加速也没有直接可迁移性。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限，但有两个泛化思路可参考：(1) "验证器引导的迭代修订"（verifier-guided revision）思想——在遥感目标检测中，可以用确定性后处理验证器（如几何约束检查）来引导检测模型的迭代优化，降低评估成本；(2) "无监督演示的强化学习训练"（GRPO + LoRA）——在遥感小样本场景下，可探索用验证器/规则提供reward信号来训练轻量检测模型，减少对标注数据的依赖。但这些均为间接启发，不构成直接可复现方法。

Abstract: We present PixCell, a neurosymbolic system in which multimodal agents convert a visually presented photonic component into a parametric program over a small domain-specific language (DSL) of geometric primitives. A system enabling deterministic visual verification renders evaluation asymmetrically cheaper than the generation attempt. While models using multi-seed sampling and iterative revision reach a mean best-turn IoU of only 0.416, multimodal agents through PixCell's interface and verifier consistently exceed 0.9 mean IoU, with scores reaching 0.974 and 0.955 across eight component targets while also satisfying source contracts. These results demonstrate that frontier multimodal agents can reliably understand and render executable parametric representations from visual targets. Using these live parameters, cross-stack studies on an interferometer reconstruct primitive programs that satisfy an 8.0 nm free spectral range target and the original footprint constraint on modeled 220-nm SOI, 400-nm SiN, and 400-nm TFLN stacks. PixCell further carries a paper-derived splitter from visual reconstruction through SOI full-wave simulation, producing symmetric propagation and balanced outputs. Finally, the same executable verifier supplies a training reward and dataset used to train a Qwen3.6-35B-A3B model with LoRA and GRPO without supervised demonstrations. On eight training-excluded paper figures, its mean champion IoU rises from 0.422 after eight initial attempts to 0.491 after three verifier-guided revision rounds. These results therefore establish a controlled framework for measuring, retargeting, and improving visual-to-parametric photonic component design.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：图像分割（image segmentation），具体为基于活动轮廓/水平集的局部Chan-Vese（LCV）模型求解。应用场景：灰度与彩色图像分割，包括医学图像（medical images）和显微图像（microscopy images）。传感器类型：摘要未说明（非遥感传感器）。目标类别：摘要未说明（非遥感目标）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及剪枝（pruning）、量化（quantization）、知识蒸馏（knowledge distillation）、NAS、轻量网络等模型压缩或加速方法。本文的"高效实现"指的是用MBO格式替代有限差分格式来加速变分模型的数值求解，属于算法层面的数值优化，而非深度学习模型轻量化。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。MBO格式本身是一种数值求解加速手段，理论上计算效率优于有限差分，但本文未给出任何面向星上/边缘部署的可行性分析。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标。仅提及应用于多种灰度与彩色图像，包括医学和显微图像。未给出定量评估指标（如Dice、IoU、分割精度等）。

Experiments: 摘要未说明具体实验结果、对比方法或消融实验。仅声称所提MBO算法在灰度与彩色图像（含医学、显微图像）上有效，但未提供定量对比数据。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/在轨目标检测方向基本无关，属于传统图像分割方法学论文；2. 摘要未提供任何定量实验结果、数据集细节或性能指标；3. 未讨论与深度学习方法的对比；4. 未涉及遥感图像特有的挑战（如复杂背景、小目标、旋转目标、多尺度等）；5. 未涉及任何部署/轻量化相关内容。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限。可能的间接启发：1. MBO格式作为一种高效的数值求解策略，其"将复杂变分问题分解为阈值化+扩散迭代"的思想，在理论上与二值化/阈值化后处理（如检测框后处理、分割掩码生成）有一定类比价值；2. 若未来研究涉及遥感图像分割（如云检测、水体分割）且采用传统变分方法，本文的MBO加速思路可参考。但整体上对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的直接可借鉴点很少。

Abstract: Robust to intensity inhomogeneity, the local Chan--Vese (LCV) model extends the classical Chan--Vese (CV) image segmentation method by incorporating local statistical information around each pixel. Originally, the LCV model was solved using a finite difference scheme, following the approach used for the CV model. As an alternative to the finite difference scheme, a more efficient algorithm based on the Merriman-Bence-Osher (MBO) scheme was later developed for the CV model. In this paper, we derive a similar MBO-based algorithm to solve the LCV model and propose an efficient implementation. The algorithm is developed for both two-phase and multiphase segmentation, and an extension to color images is also discussed. To demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach, we apply it to a variety of grayscale and color images, including medical and microscopy images.

</details>

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：ImageCLEFmed MEDIQA-CORE 2026 Task 1（脑肿瘤亚型分类任务）。评价指标：mean macro-F1。实验条件包括Fully Multimodal（全模态）和modality-dropping（模态缺失）两种设置。

Experiments: 在全模态条件下达到mean macro-F1 0.801，超过组织者baseline（0.796），在代码通过验证的队伍中排名第二。模态缺失实验表明，该方法的优势高度依赖组织病理学模态的可用性，当模态缺失时系统性能落后于baseline。

Abstract: Brain tumor diagnosis is a time-sensitive process in which patients may wait weeks for a finalized pathology report. This problem motivates automated systems that classify tumor subtype from multimodal inputs. This paper details the DS@GT ARC team's work for ImageCLEFmed MEDIQA-CORE 2026 Task~1, Brain Tumor Subtype Classification. The task evaluates three glioma classification problems: Level-1 Molecular Type, LGG vs HGG, and WHO Grade. We combine pre-extracted MRI (NeuroVFM) and histopathology (Prov-GigaPath) embeddings with free-text radiology reports. Our team explored two trimodal fusion architectures, two report encoders (RadBERT and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct), and a biologically motivated post-processing stage. We achieve a mean macro-F1 of 0.801 under the Fully Multimodal condition, exceeding the organizers' baseline of 0.796 and ranking second among the teams whose code passed verification. Additional evaluation across modality-dropping conditions shows that this advantage depends heavily on the availability of the histopathology modality, and that our system falls behind the baseline when modalities are missing. Our code is available on GitHub at https://github.com/dsgt-arc/imageclef-mediqacore-2026.

</details>


### [19] [Swimm3R: Splatting with Medium-aware SfM for Underwater 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00950)
*Minseong Kweon,Junaed Sattar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Swimm3R，一个结合medium-aware SfM与Underwater Beta Splatting的水下3D重建统一框架，通过蒸馏空气几何先验并引入Beta基元的高斯泼溅，解决水下散射与衰减导致的3D重建失败问题。

Research relevance: 弱相关（1-2分）。该论文聚焦水下3D重建（underwater 3D reconstruction），涉及SfM、Gaussian Splatting、点云恢复等，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关。既非遥感/卫星场景，也不涉及目标检测，更不涉及轻量化/部署。唯一可能的间接关联是"distills in-air geometric priors"使用了知识蒸馏（knowledge distillation）思想，但这是用于几何先验迁移而非模型压缩，借鉴意义有限。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文聚焦水下3D重建，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不涉及目标检测、遥感场景或任何部署/压缩技术，阅读优先级极低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：水下3D重建（underwater 3D reconstruction），包括structure-from-motion (SfM)和Gaussian Splatting渲染。应用场景：水下环境（underwater environments），如海底地形重建。传感器类型：水下视频/相机。目标类别：不涉及目标检测，重建对象为海底场景几何结构。

Model architecture: Swimm3R框架包含：(1) feed-forward backbone，通过蒸馏（distill）空气几何先验（in-air geometric priors）获得；(2) physics head，用于回归水下图像形成参数（underwater image-formation parameters）、相机位姿（camera poses）和恢复的点云（restored point clouds）；(3) Underwater Beta Splatting，将Gaussian splatting扩展为Beta基元（Beta primitives），并引入scattering-aware geometric gradients以稳定水下几何表示。

Lightweight method: 摘要中提到的"distills in-air geometric priors"使用了知识蒸馏（knowledge distillation）技术，但这是用于将空气域几何先验迁移到水下feed-forward backbone，属于知识/先验迁移而非模型压缩或加速。未涉及剪枝（pruning）、量化（quantization）、NAS、轻量网络设计、实时推理或边缘/星上部署等轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未提及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署相关信息。该工作面向水下3D重建，与星上部署（onboard deployment）场景无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：作者自建的Barbados水下视频数据集（Barbados underwater video dataset）。评价指标：PSNR（峰值信噪比，用于重建质量）、RRA@15和RTA@15（用于下游定位性能）。对比方法：WaterSplatting。

Experiments: 在Barbados水下数据集上，Swimm3R在挑战性散射条件下稳健恢复水下场景结构，生成连贯的海底几何。Underwater Beta Splatting相比WaterSplatting将平均PSNR提升1.47 dB，下游定位性能在RRA@15和RTA@15上分别提升2.0和2.4个百分点。

Limitations: 摘要未说明局限性。主要缺口包括：未涉及遥感/卫星场景；未涉及目标检测任务；未提供任何轻量化/部署相关信息（参数量、FLOPs、推理速度等）；数据集为自建水下视频数据，缺乏与遥感数据集的关联；蒸馏方法仅用于几何先验迁移，与模型压缩无关。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限，但可考虑以下几点：(1) 其"distill in-air geometric priors into feed-forward backbone"的蒸馏范式，在方法论层面可类比为将大模型/教师模型知识迁移到轻量学生网络，但本论文的蒸馏目标是几何先验而非检测能力，需自行迁移到目标检测蒸馏场景；(2) physics head回归物理参数的思路，可类比遥感中利用大气/辐射物理模型辅助图像恢复或检测，但与本课题轻量化主线关联较弱；(3) 总体而言，该论文对本课题（遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化）的直接可借鉴点很少。

Abstract: We propose Swimm3R, a unified framework that combines medium-aware structure-from-motion (SfM) with Underwater Beta Splatting to address scattering- and attenuation-induced failures in underwater 3D reconstruction. Swimm3R distills in-air geometric priors into a feed-forward backbone and uses a physics head to regress underwater image-formation parameters, camera poses, and restored point clouds. Additionally, we introduce Underwater Beta Splatting, which extends Gaussian splatting with Beta primitives and scattering-aware geometric gradients for stable underwater geometry representation. We further establish the Barbados underwater video dataset to demonstrate the effectiveness of our method in challenging underwater environments. On this dataset, Swimm3R robustly recovers underwater scene structure under challenging scattering conditions, yielding coherent seafloor geometry. Using these predicted point clouds, the proposed Underwater Beta Splatting improves average PSNR by $1.47$ dB over WaterSplatting while increasing downstream localization performance by $2.0$ and $2.4$ percentage points in RRA@15 and RTA@15, respectively.

</details>

*Lisa Koßmann,Ralf Bartho,Christoph Redies,Johan Wagemans*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DODA——一个面向美学研究领域的图像数据集检索Web应用，帮助研究者浏览、比较和选择合适的美学标注数据集。

Abstract: With rapid growth in the fields of empirical and computational aesthetics we have seen a vast increase in large image datasets annotated for aesthetics. As the image databases differ widely in many respects (e.g., different standards for annotation), it can be tedious to find the dataset that fits one's research needs best. The absence of a centralized open-science search system causes additional problems. Currently, researchers typically share dataset links in papers or on diverse platforms like OSF, GitHub or Dropbox. Manually searching for details like image quality and content often requires downloading all datasets. Therefore, we present the Database Of Datasets for Aesthetics (DODA), an intuitive Web application in which researchers can browse all important datasets for aesthetics research. DODA provides general information about these datasets (size, resolution, type of annotation, number of annotators, etc.) and for many of them also precomputed quantitative image properties. We discuss relevant criteria for selecting a suitable dataset with DODA and illustrate the benefits of reusing datasets. Our approach facilitates collaboration across the fields of empirical and computational aesthetics. Keywords: empirical aesthetics, computational aesthetics, machine learning, image annotation, quantitative image properties, Open Science

*Junhao Chen,Mingjin Chen,Henghaofan Zhang,Minglin Chen,Liaoyuan Fan,Boran Zhang,Saining Zhang,Mingze Sun,Hao Zhao,Ruqi Huang,Zhihao Li,Yufei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DAR，一种参考引导的4D生成渲染器，通过将神经4D G-buffer（tracking、world position、normal）注入Wan2.2视频扩散模型的control adapter，实现相机运动与场景内部动画的联合控制。

Research relevance: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关（1分）。该论文聚焦于4D视频生成渲染（4D generative rendering），属于生成式AI/视频扩散模型领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱或多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测任务，更不涉及任何模型轻量化或边缘/星上部署技术。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：4D生成渲染（4D generative rendering），即给定动画网格、相机轨迹和参考图像，用预训练视频扩散模型作为渲染器生成视频。应用场景：视频生成/动画渲染。传感器类型：不适用（非遥感传感器）。目标类别：不适用（非目标检测任务）。

Model architecture: DAR基于Wan2.2视频扩散模型，扩展其相机控制能力（从仅Plücker rays到联合camera-plus-geometry接口）。核心设计：将动画网格投影为神经4D G-buffer（包含tracking、world position、normal三个通道），通过加宽的control adapter注入模型，同时保留预训练的image-to-video先验。关键设计选择是tracking与world position的组合：tracking标识应承载外观的持久表面元素，world position给出当前场景坐标状态，normal提供局部形状。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文未涉及剪枝（pruning）、量化（quantization）、知识蒸馏（knowledge distillation）、NAS、轻量网络设计等模型压缩或加速方法。仅提到LoRA（Low-Rank Adaptation）作为参数高效微调手段，但这是训练效率优化而非推理端轻量化。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署相关信息。视频扩散模型本身计算量巨大，与星上/边缘实时部署场景完全不匹配。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：DAR-4D benchmark（68个案例）。评价指标：PSNR、SSIM、LPIPS。实验设置：LoRA微调与全量微调两种模式对比，并与off-the-shelf Wan2.2-Depth基线对比。

Experiments: 主要结果：LoRA DAR达到PSNR 23.22、SSIM 0.895、LPIPS 0.134，相比Wan2.2-Depth提升1.54 dB PSNR；全量微调达到PSNR 25.36、SSIM 0.917。消融实验：将world position替换为depth会在每个checkpoint降低PSNR 1.26-1.55 dB，验证了tracking+world position对应关系作为4D渲染条件的有效性。

Limitations: 摘要未说明局限性。但可推断：1）视频扩散模型计算开销极大，不适合实时/边缘部署；2）DAR-4D benchmark仅68个案例，规模较小；3）未涉及遥感场景或目标检测任务；4）未报告任何轻量化或部署相关指标。

Abstract: Pretrained video diffusion models can act as renderers when the desired scene state is already specified by an animated mesh, a camera trajectory, and a reference image. This 4D generative rendering setting raises a representation question: what image-format condition lets a video backbone obey both camera motion and scene-internal animation? We propose DAR, a reference-guided renderer that extends Wan2.2 camera control from Plücker rays alone to a joint camera-plus-geometry interface. DAR projects a neural 4D G-buffer (tracking, world position, and normal) from the animated mesh and injects it through a widened control adapter while preserving the pretrained image-to-video prior. The central design choice is the pair of tracking and world position. Tracking identifies the persistent surface element that should carry appearance; world position gives its current scene-coordinate state; normal supplies local shape. Depth plus calibrated rays can recover 3D in principle, but depth is a camera-dependent chart in which camera and object motion are mixed. On the 68-case DAR-4D benchmark, LoRA DAR reaches PSNR 23.22, SSIM 0.895, and LPIPS 0.134, improving over off-the-shelf Wan2.2-Depth by 1.54 dB PSNR; a full fine-tune reaches PSNR 25.36 and SSIM 0.917. Matched ablations show that replacing world position by depth reduces PSNR by 1.26--1.55 dB at every checkpoint, supporting tracking+world-position correspondence as a practical 4D rendering condition.

</details>

Abstract: Current deep learning-based character vision studies, e.g., text recognition, character image denoising, and historical text completion, are offering new solutions for learning, managing, and utilizing character resources. However, the performance of these studies peaks only with large and balanced datasets, which is a rarity with real-world character datasets, especially for logographic character languages, e.g., Chinese. The imbalance in data distribution of logographic characters is a common issue due to differences in character usage frequency and new characters being continuously created. In this paper, we propose a novel method for logographic character recognition, which introduces a multi-modal learning approach using visual semantics and contextual semantics of characters. A novel pre-training strategy is designed to enhance deep visual representations, especially for datasets suffering from issues of imbalanced and rare instances, by extracting the contextual semantics of each character from the corresponding language models. We conduct experiments across various datasets to evaluate our character recognition method and further validate the contrastive pre-training strategy by several downstream tasks. Experimental results demonstrate the superiority of our method compared to state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [23] [Fermat Active Laplace Learning for Semi-Supervised Hyperspectral Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02483)
*Vutichart Buranasiri,James M. Murphy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出两种结合密度感知Fermat距离与Poisson重加权调和标签传播的主动学习算法（FALL和A-FALL），用于高光谱图像分类中的样本标注查询。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2分）。论文涉及高光谱图像（HSI）分类，属于遥感/卫星数据范畴，但任务是对地物进行分类（classification），而非目标检测（object detection），且完全不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、蒸馏、边缘/星上部署等模型压缩或加速内容。因此与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向仅在高光谱遥感数据层面有间接关联，核心任务和方法均不匹配。

Reading priority: 2分。论文聚焦高光谱图像分类的主动学习算法，与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向仅在高光谱遥感数据层面弱相关，不涉及目标检测和模型压缩/部署，阅读价值有限。除非课题涉及高光谱分类或主动学习样本选择，否则优先级较低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：高光谱图像（HSI）分类（classification），属于像素级地物分类任务，非目标检测。应用场景：遥感高光谱影像地物分类。传感器类型：高光谱成像仪（hyperspectral imaging）。目标类别：摘要未说明具体地物类别，实验在Salinas A和Pavia数据集上进行。

Model architecture: 核心方法为主动学习（active learning）框架，包含两个算法：(1) FALL（Fermat Active Laplace Learning）：用Fermat距离构建所有数据点间的affinity matrix，再运行Poisson ReWeighted Laplace Learning（PWLL），并加入基于minimum-norm acquisition function的对角扰动；(2) A-FALL（Approximate FALL）：通过farthest-point sampling选取landmark像素，用landmark multidimensional scaling构建affinity matrix，并通过leave-one-out交叉验证选择Fermat指数p。整体属于图学习/标签传播（harmonic label propagation）方法，非深度学习网络架构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何模型压缩或加速方法（无剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。A-FALL的landmark采样可视为一种计算可扩展性优化，但属于算法层面的近似加速，而非面向部署的模型轻量化。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署指标。A-FALL通过landmark多维缩放提升了在大规模HSI场景下的可扩展性（scalability），但这是针对主动学习查询过程的计算效率，而非面向星上/边缘推理的部署优化。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Salinas A和Pavia两个高光谱影像数据集。评价指标：摘要未明确列出具体指标，但结合"labeling accuracy"表述，应为分类准确率（accuracy）类指标。实验设置：主动学习多轮查询（query rounds），A-FALL使用leave-one-out交叉验证选择Fermat指数p。

Experiments: 实验验证了FALL在Salinas A和Pavia上的有效性（effectiveness），以及A-FALL在大规模HSI场景下的可扩展性（scalability）。摘要未给出具体数值结果、对比基线或消融实验细节。

Limitations: 摘要未说明。主要缺口包括：未涉及目标检测任务；未涉及任何轻量化/部署方法；未报告计算开销、推理速度等部署相关指标；方法基于图学习，在大规模数据上即使有A-FALL近似，仍可能面临图构建和标签传播的计算瓶颈；主动学习依赖人工标注查询，与在轨全自动推理场景不匹配。

Ideas for my research: 尽管与课题核心方向弱相关，仍有少量可借鉴点：(1) Fermat距离和密度感知的流形估计思想，可用于遥感小目标检测中特征空间的密度建模或难样本挖掘；(2) A-FALL的landmark采样（farthest-point sampling）与多维缩放近似思路，可迁移到遥感大场景下特征降维或锚点选择，辅助轻量化检测器在超大影像上的高效处理；(3) 主动学习中的不确定性查询策略，可用于遥感标注样本稀缺场景下的数据增强或半监督训练。但这些均为间接启发，不构成核心借鉴。

Abstract: Two active learning algorithms for hyperspectral image (HSI) classification are proposed that combine density-aware Fermat distances with Poisson-reweighted harmonic label propagation. Our methods actively query points using an uncertainty-based acquisition function, extending Poisson ReWeighted Laplace Learning (PWLL). Our first algorithm, Fermat Active Laplace Learning (FALL), builds an affinity matrix using Fermat distances between all data points. Then, PWLL is run with a diagonal perturbation using the minimum-norm acquisition function. In contrast, Approximate FALL (A-FALL) computes Fermat distances between each data point and landmark pixels selected via farthest-point sampling and constructs the affinity matrix using landmark multidimensional scaling. After several query rounds, A-FALL selects the Fermat exponent $p$ using a leave-one-out cross-validation variant. FALL and A-FALL leverage Fermat distances and subsequent harmonic label propagation to provide a density-aware estimation of the data manifold, improving labeling accuracy. Experiments on Salinas A and Pavia show the effectiveness of FALL and the scalability of A-FALL to large HSI scenes.

</details>

*Satvik Tripathi,Mustafa Ege Seker,Kristian Quevada,Ebubechukwu D Enwerem,Pratham Khandelwal,Emine Meltem,Bera Koca,Shahriar Faghani,Jacinta Arnold,Dania Daye,Tessa S. Cook*
