Abstract: Bandit algorithms generate data for downstream inference, but adaptive sampling biases post-bandit sample means. We analyze this bias for stable index algorithms, including UCB1 and its generalizations, and derive sharp leading-order expressions for the sample-mean bias and expected $Z$-statistic. Our characterization reveals the algorithmic origin of bias through a key index-function-dependent quantity, which we term effective exploration rate. For example, under UCB1, the effective exploration rate is of order $\sqrt{\log T}$, and the standardized bias of any arm (that is not uniquely optimal) decays at the extremely slow rate $1/\sqrt{\log T}$. We also show how the choice of the index function affects both regret and bias, which reveals a regret-bias trade-off: more exploratory algorithm reduces bias but increases regret. Our sharp characterization for bias uses a novel empirical fluid approximation of the algorithm's sampling dynamics, which may be of independent interest.

</details>

TL;DR: 提出一种面向实时受监管场景的大语言模型（LLM）运维（LLMOps）架构，整合实时数据摄取、持续学习、检索增强生成（RAG）和人在回路反馈，以解决知识过时、灾难性遗忘、幻觉和反馈闭环弱等问题。

Research relevance: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦大语言模型（LLM）的运维架构（LLMOps），涉及持续学习、RAG、RLHF等NLP/LLM领域技术，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱或空间在轨智能。也不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测或遥感场景理解。轻量化方面仅涉及延迟-成本-精度权衡（latency-cost-accuracy trade-off），但并非面向边缘/星上部署的模型压缩方法。相关性评分：1分。

Reading priority: 1分。论文聚焦LLMOps架构，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关。仅个别持续学习/资源调度思想可间接借鉴，不建议作为核心阅读文献。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：大语言模型（LLM）运维（LLMOps）架构设计与评估。应用场景：实时、受监管的高风险行业（如医疗健康、金融）中的LLM部署。传感器类型：不适用（非遥感/视觉任务）。目标类别：不适用（非目标检测任务）。

Model architecture: 提出统一、模式驱动的LLMOps架构，包含四个核心组件：(1) AIPO（Adaptive Ingestion Pattern Orchestrator）自适应数据摄取模式编排器，用FreshStreamBench评估；(2) STAR+FAR持续学习方法，采用稀疏时间适配器路由（sparse temporal adapter routing）和新鲜度感知回放（freshness-aware replay）；(3) SAGE，一种SLO感知的自适应检索策略（SLO-aware adaptive retrieval policy），预测每次查询的passage预算以满足尾部延迟目标；(4) 自动化反馈驱动的收敛阶段，带RLHF触发机制。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等部署指标。仅提及"reduces latency-cost-accuracy trade-offs"（降低延迟-成本-精度权衡），但这是针对云端LLM服务场景的SLO（Service Level Objective）优化，而非面向星上/边缘硬件（FPGA/NPU/TensorRT）的部署可行性分析。不涉及实时推理的硬件约束或资源受限环境。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要提及FreshStreamBench作为AIPO组件的评估基准。其他数据集未说明。评价指标涉及延迟（latency）、成本（cost）、精度（accuracy）、尾部延迟目标（tail-latency targets），但具体数值和指标定义摘要未说明。

Experiments: 摘要未提供具体实验结果、对比方法或消融实验细节。仅声明该架构"reduces latency-cost-accuracy trade-offs while supporting auditability and rollback"，但缺乏定量数据支撑。

Limitations: 摘要未说明局限性。主要缺口包括：无定量实验结果；未涉及遥感/视觉任务；未提供模型压缩或边缘部署相关指标；应用场景（医疗、金融）与遥感在轨智能差异巨大；未讨论计算资源受限环境下的可行性。

Ideas for my research: 尽管整体弱相关，但个别思想可借鉴：(1) STAR+FAR中的"freshness-aware replay"（新鲜度感知回放）思想，可类比遥感在轨场景中模型持续学习时对新增/变化地物样本的优先级采样策略；(2) SAGE的SLO感知自适应检索策略中"per-query budget预测"思想，可类比在轨推理时根据计算预算动态调整检测模型推理深度或输入分辨率；(3) 稀疏时间适配器路由（sparse temporal adapter routing）的稀疏参数更新思想，对在轨模型的增量更新/参数高效微调有参考价值。但这些均为间接借鉴，需自行映射到遥感目标检测与轻量化场景。

Abstract: Large language models deployed in real-time, regulated settings face knowledge staleness, catastrophic forgetting, hallucination, and weak feedback loops. We present a unified, pattern-driven LLMOps architecture integrating real-time data ingestion, continual learning, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), and human-in-the-loop feedback into a single operational pipeline. Four contributions map to established software design patterns: an adaptive ingestion pattern orchestrator (AIPO) evaluated with FreshStreamBench; STAR+FAR continual learning with sparse temporal adapter routing and freshness-aware replay; SAGE, an SLO-aware adaptive retrieval policy predicting a per-query passage budget to meet tail-latency targets; and an automated feedback-driven convergence stage with RLHF triggers. The result reduces latency-cost-accuracy trade-offs while supporting auditability and rollback for high-risk sectors such as health care and finance.

</details>


### [51] [Stochastic Sequential Search in Very-High-Dimensional Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01502)
*Petr Somol,Jiří Grim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种预算受限的采样算子对（budgeted sampled step operator pair），将传统顺序特征选择方法（如 floating search）从全量候选扫描改为固定数量的候选评估，形成随机顺序搜索（Stochastic Sequential Search, SSS）家族，使特征选择在高维问题上保持质量的同时大幅降低计算成本。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2分）。该论文属于特征选择（feature selection）领域的泛机器学习方法，与遥感、卫星、在轨目标检测均无直接关联。虽然特征选择可作为一种模型压缩/轻量化的辅助手段，但论文本身不涉及遥感场景、目标检测或任何部署相关技术，属于泛机器学习方法。

Reading priority: 2分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向关联较弱，属于泛机器学习特征选择方法。仅在 NAS 搜索策略和特征/通道筛选的采样机制上可能有间接借鉴价值，但优先级较低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：特征选择（feature selection），具体为顺序特征选择中的 floating search 方法的随机化加速。应用场景：高维数据特征筛选（如 madelon、gisette、reuters 数据集）。传感器类型：不适用（非遥感）。目标类别：不适用（非目标检测）。

Model architecture: 提出 Stochastic Sequential Search (SSS) 家族，核心是 budgeted sampled step operator pair：用固定数量的候选评估替代全量候选扫描；候选通过温度控制的 softmax 采样从依赖感知的逐特征统计量中抽取，并配以均匀探索下限（uniform exploration floor）。具体研究了 floating search 的随机对应物 sSFFS（stochastic Sequential Floating Forward Search）。

Lightweight method: 本文并非传统意义上的模型轻量化方法（如 pruning、quantization、knowledge distillation、NAS），而是通过随机采样算子降低特征选择搜索的计算复杂度——将每步候选评估次数从 O(d)（d为特征维度）降为固定预算，使每步计算成本与维度无关。这属于算法层面的计算加速，而非模型压缩。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文未涉及星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署指标。仅在 reuters 数据集上提到约两分钟的单核评估时间，但这是特征选择搜索过程的时间，而非推理部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：madelon（500维）、gisette（5000维）、reuters（10105维）。评价指标：criterion value（特征选择准则值）、holdout accuracy（留出集准确率）、评估次数（evaluations）。对比方法：full-SFFS、DAF、BIF ranking、uniform sampling。

Experiments: 1) 在500维 madelon 上，sSFFS 在约1/4的评估次数下保留 full-SFFS 准则值的至少97%；2) 在5000维 gisette 上，sSFFS 在匹配预算下超过 DAF 和 BIF ranking 的饱和准则水平；500个训练样本时，顺序搜索前沿之外的约束变为准则本身而非搜索；3) 在10105维 reuters 上，sSFFS 在搜索目标和留出准确率上均优于 BIF 和 DAF，约两分钟单核完成。

Limitations: 摘要未说明。但可推断：1) 论文完全面向特征选择任务，未涉及任何遥感/目标检测/部署场景；2) 未报告模型推理的参数量、FLOPs、延迟等部署指标；3) 随机采样引入的方差和稳定性问题未在摘要中讨论；4) 温度参数、探索下限等超参数的选择策略未说明。

Ideas for my research: 尽管与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向弱相关，但有以下可借鉴点：1) 预算受限的采样搜索思想可迁移到 NAS（Neural Architecture Search）中——在星上资源受限条件下，用固定预算的随机采样替代全量架构搜索，降低搜索成本；2) 依赖感知的逐特征统计量在线学习机制，可用于遥感高光谱/多光谱波段的特征筛选，辅助轻量化模型输入通道选择；3) 温度控制的 softmax 采样 + 均匀探索下限的平衡策略，可用于剪枝（pruning）中通道/滤波器重要性采样的探索-利用权衡。

Abstract: Sequential subset search -- forward selection with floating backtracking and its descendants -- remains the quality reference in feature selection, but every member of the family sweeps the full pool of remaining candidate features at each step, which excludes it from very-high-dimensional problems; there, only individual-feature ranking remains practical, and it models feature interplay weakly or not at all. We introduce a budgeted sampled step operator pair that replaces the full sweeps by a fixed number of candidate evaluations per step. Candidates are drawn by temperature-controlled softmax sampling from dependency-aware per-feature statistics learned online from every criterion evaluation the search performs, guarded by a uniform exploration floor; per-step cost becomes independent of dimensionality. Substituting the operators turns any sequential method into its stochastic counterpart, defining the Stochastic Sequential Search (SSS) family; we study the stochastic counterpart of floating search, sSFFS. On 500-dimensional madelon, sSFFS retains at least 97% of the full-SFFS criterion value at every subset size at about a quarter of its evaluations, while uniform sampling at the same budget collapses on madelon's synergistic features. On 5,000-dimensional gisette, far beyond full-SFFS reach, sSFFS exceeds the saturated criterion level of DAF and BIF ranking at matched budgets; holdout validation shows that at 500 training samples the binding constraint beyond the sequential frontier becomes the criterion, not the search. On 10,105-dimensional reuters, under a trustworthy multinomial filter criterion, sSFFS dominates BIF and DAF on the search objective and on holdout accuracy at every subset size, in about two minutes of single-core evaluation work. A verified standalone implementation accompanies the paper.

</details>


### [52] [HP-JEPA: Hierarchical Partitioning for Multi-Resolution Graph Joint-Embedding Predictive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00491)
*Ruichen Xu,Jingxiang Qu,Wenhan Gao,Jiaxing Zhang,Linsey Pang,Ravid Shwartz-Ziv,Yann LeCun,Yuefan Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HP-JEPA，一种基于层级多分辨率图划分的联合嵌入预测架构，通过在不同粒度划分下分别进行潜在空间预测并整合多尺度表示，提升图自监督表示学习的迁移能力。

Research relevance: 与本研究方向弱相关（2分）。论文属于图自监督学习（graph self-supervised learning）领域，核心是图神经网络表示学习，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱或多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测任务，更不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS或边缘/星上部署。属于泛机器学习/图表示学习方法，对遥感目标检测和轻量化方向仅有非常间接的方法论参考价值。

Reading priority: 1分。论文属于图自监督学习领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测和模型轻量化方向基本无关，不涉及遥感场景、目标检测或任何模型压缩/部署技术。仅当研究涉及图结构建模的遥感场景理解时，可作为方法论参考，但优先级很低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：图自监督表示学习（graph self-supervised learning），具体为graph classification和graph regression。应用场景：通用图数据（非遥感场景）。传感器类型：不适用（非遥感）。目标类别：不适用（非目标检测）。

Model architecture: HP-JEPA采用joint-embedding predictive architecture (JEPA)框架，包含：online encoder、exponential-moving-average (EMA) target encoder、latent predictor。核心创新是将图组织成coarse-to-fine的多分辨率划分（hierarchical partitioning），在每个分辨率上分别进行context-target latent prediction，最后通过concatenation或task-specific resolution weighting整合各分辨率表示。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何模型压缩或加速方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署相关信息。且该方法是自监督预训练方法，本身面向离线训练场景，与星上实时推理部署无直接关联。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：7个graph classification benchmarks和1个graph regression benchmark（具体数据集名称摘要未说明）。评价指标：graph classification准确率（accuracy）和graph regression误差指标（具体指标名称摘要未说明）。实验设置：与固定分辨率Graph-JEPA基线对比，并进行了size-stratified分析（按图大小四分位分层评估）。

Experiments: 在8个任务中，HP-JEPA在6个任务上优于固定分辨率的Graph-JEPA基线；在3个代表性数据集的大多数图大小四分位上取得更高准确率。摘要未说明与其他SOTA图自监督方法的对比结果，也未提供消融实验细节。

Limitations: 1. 摘要未说明模型复杂度、训练开销和推理效率；2. 未报告与更广泛图自监督SOTA方法的全面对比；3. 多分辨率划分的计算开销可能较大，但摘要未讨论；4. 未涉及遥感场景或目标检测任务，适用性需另行验证；5. 未说明resolution weighting的具体学习机制和超参数敏感性。

Ideas for my research: 尽管论文与遥感目标检测和轻量化无直接关系，但有以下间接借鉴点：1. 多尺度/多分辨率特征融合思想可迁移到遥感小目标检测中——遥感图像中目标尺度差异大，借鉴"coarse-to-fine多分辨率划分+任务特定加权融合"的思路，可设计多尺度特征金字塔的加权融合策略；2. EMA target encoder的稳定训练机制可借鉴到遥感自监督预训练中，用于在无标注遥感影像上预训练检测骨干网络；3. 若未来将遥感场景建模为图结构（如场景图、目标关系图），该多分辨率图表示方法可用于遥感场景理解中的图级任务。

Abstract: Graph self-supervised learning aims to learn transferable representations from large-scale unlabeled graph data. Joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs) avoid explicit negative-pair construction and raw-input reconstruction by predicting masked targets directly in latent space. However, existing graph JEPAs typically rely on a single predefined graph partition, biasing the learned representations toward one structural granularity and limiting their ability to capture complementary patterns at different graph scales. To address this limitation, we propose HP-JEPA, a hierarchical partitioning framework for multi-resolution graph joint-embedding prediction. HP-JEPA organizes each graph into an ordered bank of coarse-to-fine partition resolutions and performs context-target latent prediction separately at each resolution using an online encoder, an exponential-moving-average target encoder, and a latent predictor. The resulting resolution-specific graph representations are subsequently integrated through concatenation or task-specific resolution weighting, allowing downstream models to combine complementary local, regional, and global structural information. Experiments on seven graph classification benchmarks and one graph regression benchmark show that HP-JEPA outperforms the fixed-resolution Graph-JEPA baseline on 6 of 8 tasks, improving upon Graph-JEPA on most evaluated benchmarks. Size-stratified analyses further show that HP-JEPA achieves higher accuracy than Graph-JEPA in most evaluated graph-size quartiles on three representative datasets. These results highlight the effectiveness of hierarchical multi-resolution partitioning for transferable graph representation learning.

</details>

*Zejun Xie,Xintong Li,Guang Wang,Desheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Aggregate-then-Calibrate (AtC)两阶段框架，将人类比较判断聚合为共识排序，再通过isotonic calibration校准预测模型分数，用于缺乏可验证真值的人类中心评估任务。

Research relevance: 弱相关（1-2分）。该论文研究的是human-centered assessment tasks（人类中心评估任务），属于判断聚合（judgment aggregation）与模型校准（model calibration）领域，与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化部署均无直接关联。既非遥感场景，也非目标检测任务，更不涉及任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Reading priority: 1分。论文主题（人类中心评估与判断聚合）与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不涉及遥感、目标检测、轻量化或部署中的任何核心要素，无需优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：人类中心评估（human-centered assessment），如系统化决策中的主观评分任务。应用场景：需要人类判断且缺乏可验证ground truth的评估场景。传感器类型：不适用（非遥感/视觉任务）。目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: AtC框架包含两个阶段：Stage-1使用rank-aggregation model（排名聚合模型）聚合异质注释者的比较判断，生成共识排序（consensus ranking），并建模注释者可靠性（annotator reliability）；Stage-2通过isotonic projection（保序投影）将任意预测模型的分数校准到该排序上，在保持序一致性的同时尽可能保留模型的定量信息。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。摘要中未提及任何剪枝（pruning）、量化（quantization）、知识蒸馏（knowledge distillation）、NAS、轻量网络设计或边缘/星上部署相关的模型压缩与加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文未涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息，与星上/边缘部署完全无关。

Datasets and metrics: 使用semi-synthetic和real-world数据集进行实验。评价指标包括accuracy和robustness。具体数据集名称、规模、任务细节摘要未说明。

Experiments: 主要实验结论：AtC在semi-synthetic和real-world数据集上一致优于仅人类（human-only）或仅模型（model-only）的评估方法。理论结果包括：(1) 建模注释者异质性比同质性假设获得更高效的共识估计；(2) isotonic calibration在共识排序误设时仍具有风险界；(3) AtC渐近优于纯模型评估。具体对比方法和消融实验细节摘要未说明。

Limitations: 与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于人类判断聚合与模型校准的理论与方法，不涉及任何遥感图像、目标检测、模型轻量化或部署内容。其方法论（rank aggregation + isotonic calibration）属于统计学习与决策科学范畴，对遥感在轨目标检测轻量化课题几乎没有直接借鉴价值。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴价值有限。唯一可能的间接启发是：isotonic calibration（保序校准）思想在模型后处理阶段可用于改善检测置信度校准，但这对遥感在轨目标检测轻量化课题的贡献非常边缘。不建议将其作为主要参考。

Abstract: Human-centered assessment tasks, which are essential for systematic decision-making, rely heavily on human judgment and typically lack verifiable ground truth. Existing approaches face a dilemma: methods using only human judgments suffer from heterogeneous expertise and inconsistent rating scales, while methods using only model-generated scores must learn from imperfect proxies or incomplete features. We propose Aggregate-then-Calibrate (AtC), a two-stage framework that combines these complementary sources. Stage-1 aggregates heterogeneous comparative judgments into a consensus ranking using a rank-aggregation model that accounts for annotator reliability. Stage-2 calibrates any predictive model's scores by an isotonic projection onto the order, enforcing ordinal consistency while preserving as much of the model's quantitative information as possible. Theoretically, we show: (1) modeling annotator heterogeneity yields strictly more efficient consensus estimation than homogeneity; (2) isotonic calibration enjoys risk bounds even when the consensus ranking is misspecified; and (3) AtC asymptotically outperforms model-only assessment. Across semi-synthetic and real-world datasets, AtC consistently improves accuracy and robustness over human-only or model-only assessments. Our results bridge judgment aggregation with model-free calibration, providing a principled recipe for human-centered assessment when ground truth is costly, scarce, or unverifiable.

</details>


### [54] [Agentic Graph Token Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00542)
*Zhuoyi Peng,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出agentic graph token reasoning方法，将图token化作为大语言模型推理过程的一部分，通过三步训练流程让模型逐步选择图视图并生成轨迹相关的图token，在七个图域上大幅超越基线并实现零样本迁移。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2分）。论文聚焦于LLM-based图分析（graph analysis），属于泛机器学习/泛AI方法，与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化部署均无直接关联。虽然图token推理方法在理论上可能对遥感场景图结构建模有间接启发，但摘要中完全没有涉及遥感图像、目标检测或模型压缩/加速内容。

Reading priority: 1分。论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，属于LLM图分析领域的前沿工作。除非对动态推理/条件计算在检测模型加速中的应用感兴趣，否则优先级很低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：图数据分析（graph analysis），包括节点分类、图级推理等；应用场景：引文网络、产品共购图等通用图数据；传感器类型：不适用（非遥感传感器）；目标类别：不适用（非目标检测任务）。

Model architecture: 基于LLM的图分析框架：图编码器（graph encoder）将图视图（节点、k-hop邻域、簇）压缩为连续token块；模型在推理过程中动态选择图视图和粒度，图编码器按需生成图token并拼接到运行上下文。采用三步训练流程：(i)自监督任务学习读取异构图token；(ii)token-robust轨迹阶段，带图token一致性正则化；(iii)偏好优化，奖励图token证据与节点文本证据一致的轨迹。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS或轻量网络设计等模型压缩/加速方法。虽然"on-demand graph encoding"在概念上减少了不必要的计算，但并非面向部署的轻量化设计。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署相关信息。且该方法基于LLM，计算开销通常较大，与星上/边缘部署的轻量化需求方向相反。

Datasets and metrics: 跨越七个图域（seven graph domains）的评估，具体数据集名称摘要未说明；评价指标摘要未说明；包含零样本迁移到未见域的实验设置。

Experiments: 在七个图域上大幅超越多种基线（baselines），并能在无需per-target微调的情况下零样本迁移到未见域。具体对比方法、消融实验细节摘要未说明。

Limitations: 摘要未说明。但可推断：1) 基于LLM的方法计算开销大，不适合资源受限的星上部署；2) 图token推理的轨迹依赖特性可能引入推理延迟；3) 与遥感图像目标检测任务无直接关联，缺乏视觉/图像域验证。

Ideas for my research: 尽管与遥感目标检测轻量化方向弱相关，但有两个概念可借鉴：1) "on-demand encoding"思想——在推理时按需激活/计算，而非一次性全量计算，可类比于动态推理（dynamic inference）或条件计算（conditional computation）用于遥感目标检测模型的推理加速；2) "token-robust trajectory"与一致性正则化思想，可用于知识蒸馏中教师-学生模型的一致性约束设计。但这些仅为方法论层面的间接启发，需自行迁移到遥感检测场景。

Abstract: Graphs model relational data throughout science and industry, from citation networks to product co-purchase graphs. Because the nodes of many such graphs carry rich text, a growing line of work applies large language models (LLMs) to graph analysis. The most graph-native of these methods use graph tokens: a graph encoder compresses a graph view, such as a node, its k-hop neighbourhood, or a cluster, into a short block of continuous tokens that jointly encodes node attributes and topology and is read directly by the model. Existing methods, however, use graph tokens in a static single-shot manner: they encode one predefined graph view before the model has even seen the target and never revise it, leaving the model's step-by-step reasoning ability unused. We introduce agentic graph token reasoning, which recasts graph tokenization as part of the reasoning process itself. At each step, the model chooses which graph view to encode and at what granularity; a graph encoder is invoked on demand to materialise the corresponding graph tokens; and the resulting block is spliced into the running context. The model thus reasons step by step in the graph token space, and the tokens it reads are trajectory-dependent. We realise this with a three-stage training pipeline: (i) self-supervised tasks that teach the model to read heterogeneous graph tokens, (ii) a token-robust trajectory stage with a graph-token consistency regulariser, and (iii) preference optimisation that rewards trajectories in which the graph-token evidence and the node-text evidence agree. Across evaluations spanning seven graph domains, our models outperform a broad set of baselines by a large margin and transfer zero-shot to unseen domains without any per-target fine-tuning. More broadly, this work pushes LLM-based graph analysis from static graph-token encoders towards a graph-native agent paradigm.

</details>


### [55] [Crushing the Evidence: A Dual-Penalty Evasion Framework for Fooling White-Box Explainable AI Auditors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00566)
*Niraj Kumar,Harsh Kasyap*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种白盒、梯度正则化的对抗性解释逃避攻击框架，通过连续嵌入双惩罚机制在训练时将触发特征梯度直接惩罚进模型参数，从而在不依赖OOD脚手架包装的情况下生成平滑的分布内预测，绕过条件异常检测，使目标特征归因趋近于零。

Research relevance: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关（1分）。论文聚焦于机器学习可解释性（explainability）管道的对抗性攻击与防御，属于AI安全/可解释性领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等任何遥感相关场景，也不涉及object detection、轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、边缘部署等任何目标检测或模型压缩技术。

Reading priority: 1分。论文属于AI安全/可解释性对抗攻击领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入阅读时间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：对抗性解释逃避攻击（adversarial explanation evasion attack）与防御；应用场景：金融、医疗、社会福利等高风险敏感领域的高风险模型审计；传感器类型：不适用（使用表格数据）；目标类别：不适用（针对模型特征归因/可解释性输出）。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。方法核心是"continuous-embedding dual-penalty framework"（连续嵌入双惩罚框架），在训练过程中直接惩罚触发特征梯度（trigger feature gradients），将逃避逻辑原生嵌入模型参数中，而非依赖OOD scaffolding wrappers。

Lightweight method: 不涉及轻量化方法。摘要中未提及pruning、quantization、knowledge distillation、NAS、轻量网络、实时推理、边缘部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等任何模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及星上/边缘部署，未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：四个基准表格数据集（tabular datasets），包括COMPAS、German Credit、IEEE-CIS、Communities & Crime。评价指标：Attack Success Rates（攻击成功率，>90%）、目标特征归因值（target feature attribution，被压制到<0.02）、绕过Conditional Anomaly Detection的能力。

Experiments: 在四个表格数据集上的实证评估表明：该方法能将目标特征归因系统性压制到接近零（<0.02），维持超过90%的攻击成功率，并从根本上绕过条件异常检测（Conditional Anomaly Detection）。摘要未提供与已有攻击/防御方法的详细对比表格或消融实验细节。

Limitations: 1. 仅针对表格数据（tabular data）验证，未涉及图像/遥感影像场景；2. 摘要未说明方法的计算开销、训练成本或对模型精度的影响；3. 未讨论该方法在深度神经网络（如CNN/Transformer）上的可迁移性；4. 未涉及任何遥感目标检测或模型轻量化相关内容；5. 摘要未说明方法的泛化能力边界及防御侧可能的对抗策略。

Ideas for my research: 虽然论文与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向基本无关，但存在一个间接可借鉴点：其"将逻辑原生嵌入模型参数"的思想与知识蒸馏/模型压缩中"将教师知识嵌入学生网络参数"的思路有概念上的相似性。此外，其"continuous-embedding dual-penalty framework"中通过梯度惩罚约束模型行为的正则化思想，可类比于轻量化训练中通过正则化约束稀疏性或量化误差的思路。但整体而言，对本课题的可借鉴性非常有限。

Abstract: Post-hoc model explainers such as LIME, SHAP, and Integrated Gradients are widely deployed to audit models in high-stakes sensitive domains, including finance, healthcare, and social welfare. This ensures the model's transparency and acceptability. However, a few studies have examined potential attacks in the explainability pipeline. Adversaries can attempt to conceal algorithmic biases or backdoors using adversarial explanation attacks. These attacks have relied on scaffolding out-of-distribution (OOD) detectors that toggle predictions when queried by an explainer. Consequently, defenses have been developed to successfully neutralize these black-box attacks by identifying their anomalous perturbation footprints. In this paper, we demonstrate a critical vulnerability by introducing a more potent white-box, gradient-regularized evasion attack framework. By employing a continuous-embedding dual-penalty framework, we directly penalize trigger feature gradients during training on in-distribution data. Since our approach embeds the evasion logic natively into the model parameters, without relying on OOD scaffolding wrappers, it generates smooth, in-distribution predictions that leave no anomaly footprint. Empirical evaluations across four benchmark tabular datasets (COMPAS, German Credit, IEEE-CIS, and Communities & Crime) confirm that our method systematically crushes target feature attribution to near-zero (<0.02), maintains >90% Attack Success Rates, and fundamentally bypasses Conditional Anomaly Detection.

</details>

*Shengzhi Deng,Chenqi Ye,Yanze Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究伪随机数生成器（PRNG）的轨道结构如何作为可学习输入影响扩散模型的训练与生成质量，提出MLP预测器和扩散探针两种度量方法，发现伪随机源不仅是分布选择，更是模型依赖的结构化输入。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文研究扩散模型（diffusion models）中伪随机数生成器（pseudorandom number generator）的轨道结构对训练和生成质量的影响，属于生成式模型与随机数理论交叉领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。唯一可能的间接关联是扩散模型在遥感图像生成/超分中有应用，但本论文聚焦于随机数源本身，而非遥感场景。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：扩散模型训练与生成质量分析；应用场景：通用图像生成（MNIST、CIFAR-10）；传感器类型：不适用（非遥感传感器）；目标类别：不适用（非目标检测任务）。

Model architecture: 涉及两类模型：(1) 小型多层感知机（MLP），用于从轨道历史预测下一个伪随机值，衡量序列可预测性；(2) 扩散模型（diffusion model）作为目标系统，用在线随机张量替换真实图像，保持扩散架构和训练目标不变，作为"扩散探针"（diffusion probe）衡量系统能否利用轨道结构。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未提供参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署相关信息。虽然提及"有限精度硬件"（finite-precision hardware），但这是作为伪随机数实现背景，而非部署优化讨论。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：MNIST和CIFAR-10（均为通用图像数据集，非遥感数据集）。评价指标：扩散损失（diffusion loss）、生成质量（generation quality）、秩相关（rank correlations）、经验幂律（empirical power law）指数。实验设置：控制边际统计量、筛选动力学和有限精度失败案例后，比较不同伪随机轨道下的扩散损失和生成质量。

Experiments: 主要发现：(1) 不同伪随机轨道在MNIST和CIFAR-10上产生显著不同的扩散损失和生成质量；(2) MLP预测器和扩散探针两种度量与宏观生成退化有强秩相关，但局部排序不同；(3) 经IID基线归一化后，探针损失与真实数据扩散损失近似遵循经验幂律，两个数据集上指数不同。结论：伪随机源不仅是分布选择，更是模型依赖的结构化输入。

Limitations: 摘要未说明具体局限性。但从内容看：(1) 仅验证了MNIST和CIFAR-10两个小规模通用数据集，未涉及遥感等复杂场景；(2) 未讨论对目标检测任务的影响；(3) 未涉及任何轻量化或部署优化；(4) 伪随机数对扩散模型的影响在真实大规模数据集上是否成立未知。

Ideas for my research: 尽管与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向弱相关，但有一个潜在借鉴点：如果未来在星上部署扩散模型（如遥感图像超分、去噪、生成），星上硬件（FPGA/NPU）的有限精度特性会影响伪随机数生成，进而影响扩散模型的生成质量。这提示在星上部署扩散模型时，需要关注随机数源的选择与硬件精度匹配问题。但该关联非常间接，且当前课题聚焦于目标检测而非生成模型，借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Diffusion models rely on stochastic inputs, yet on finite-precision hardware, the "randomness" they consume is realized as deterministic numerical orbits generated by pseudorandom rules. Accessible orbit structure can become a learnable input and affect both training and generation because the realized loss and its gradient depend on the concrete pseudorandom values consumed at each optimization step. A small multilayer perceptron predicts the next value of an orbit from its recent history, measuring general sequence predictability. A diffusion probe replaces real images with online random tensors while preserving the diffusion architecture and training objective, measuring whether the target system can exploit orbit structure. After controlling marginal statistics and screening out clear dynamical and finite-precision failures, the remaining orbits still produce markedly different diffusion losses and generation quality on MNIST and CIFAR-10. Both measures show strong rank correlations with macroscopic generation degradation, although their local rankings differ. After normalization by the IID baseline, the probe loss and the real-data diffusion loss approximately follow an empirical power law, with different exponents on the two datasets. These results suggest that a pseudorandom source is not only a distributional choice, but also a model-dependent structured input.

</details>

*Rachit Verma,Padala Manisha,Sujit Gujar*

Experiments: 摘要提到使用简单的审计集构建启发式方法（audit-set construction heuristics）配合线性分类器和神经网络分类器进行经验验证，展示了增加审计资源会减少但不会消除审计后操纵的空间。摘要未给出具体数值结果或对比实验细节。

Abstract: Fairness audits are increasingly mandated in high-stakes applications such as hiring, lending, and automated decision-making. Recent work has established fundamental impossibility results for black-box fairness auditing, showing that sufficiently expressive models can evade any auditing strategy. We complement these results by quantifying the extent of unavoidable post-audit manipulation under finite audit resources. We formulate fairness auditing as a min-max optimization between a computationally unbounded company and a budget-constrained auditor. We study two auditing regimes: (i) a budgeted auditor that certifies fairness using a fixed-size audit set, and (ii) a budgeted α-tolerant auditor that additionally requires the audit set to estimate the fairness of the certified model within an α approximation. For both settings, we derive explicit lower bounds on the worst-case post-audit demographic parity deviation as functions of the audit budget, group imbalance, and fairness tolerance. Finally, we empirically illustrate these theoretical limits using simple audit-set construction heuristics with linear and neural network classifiers. Our results demonstrate that increasing audit resources reduces, but does not eliminate, the scope for post-audit manipulation, highlighting fundamental limitations of finite-budget fairness certification.

</details>


### [58] [CoSynFlow: Conformal Symplectic Neural Flows for Cross-System Prediction of Dissipative Hamiltonian Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00571)
*Baige Xu,Takaharu Yaguchi*

TL;DR: 提出CoSynFlow，一种共形辛神经流模型，用于学习耗散哈密顿动力学的连续时间解算子，通过构造保持共形辛结构。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Experiments: 摘要提到CoSynFlow在结构误差上达到机器精度，取得最低的长期误差，并支持物理信息训练。但未给出具体对比方法名称和实验细节。

Abstract: Learning solution operators for differential equations is a central problem in scientific machine learning. However, many neural operator methods optimize prediction accuracy without explicitly enforcing the geometric structure of the dynamics. Structure-preserving models such as SympNets and Symplectic Neural Flows address this issue for conservative Hamiltonian systems by preserving the symplectic form. In dissipative Hamiltonian systems with conformal symplectic structure, however, the symplectic form evolves according to a conformal factor determined by the dissipation. We propose CoSynFlow, a conformal symplectic neural flow for learning continuous-time solution maps of dissipative Hamiltonian dynamics. CoSynFlow composes symplectic shear maps with explicit conformal scaling, preserving the conformal symplectic structure by construction. By conditioning it on a finite-dimensional Hamiltonian descriptor and the dissipation parameter, a single trained model predicts solution maps for unseen systems without retraining. CoSynFlow keeps the structure error at machine precision, attains the lowest long-horizon error, and admits physics-informed training.

</details>


### [59] [From field-scale to large-scale spectral libraries: Tabular foundation models in soil spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00608)
*Viacheslav Barkov,Jonas Schmidinger,Robin Gebbers,Martin Atzmueller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 系统比较了TabPFN、CNN、Cubist、Random Forest和PLSR在85个土壤光谱回归任务上的性能，发现TabPFN在完整光谱上即超越经典基线，且PLS降维与TabPFN结合效果最佳。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2分）。论文属于土壤光谱学/化学计量学（chemometrics）领域，涉及回归任务和降维方法（PCA、PLS），与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。虽然光谱数据与遥感高光谱有一定概念关联，但任务类型（回归预测土壤属性 vs 目标检测）、应用场景（土壤制图 vs 卫星在轨智能）均不同，且不涉及任何目标检测或模型压缩/部署方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：回归任务（soil property prediction），非目标检测。应用场景：field-scale digital soil mapping 和 global soil spectral library。传感器类型：vis-NIR 和 MIR 光谱仪（非遥感卫星传感器）。目标类别：土壤属性（非目标检测类别）。

Model architecture: 比较了多种模型：TabPFN（in-context learning tabular foundation model）、CNN（卷积神经网络）、Cubist（rule-based regression）、Random Forest、PLSR（partial least squares regression）。降维方法包括PCA和PLS latent variables。

Lightweight method: 摘要未明确涉及模型轻量化。仅涉及数据降维（PCA、PLS latent variables），这是特征降维而非模型压缩。未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署信息。TabPFN作为tabular foundation model通常计算开销较大，但摘要未提供具体部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：85个回归任务，来自pedometrics领域的open benchmark datasets，涵盖field-scale digital soil mapping和global soil spectral library（含数万土壤样本）。评价指标：摘要未明确说明具体指标名称（如RMSE、R²等未提及）。

Experiments: 主要发现：1) TabPFN在完整光谱上即超越所有经典基线，说明显式降维并非强性能的必要条件；2) PLS对所有模型都是有效的降维策略；3) PLS latent variables + TabPFN组合获得最佳预测性能。未提供具体数值结果。

Limitations: 1) 任务为回归而非目标检测，与遥感在轨目标检测无直接关联；2) 未涉及任何模型压缩或边缘/星上部署；3) 摘要未提供具体性能数值、模型规模或推理效率数据；4) 土壤光谱数据与遥感影像数据在维度、空间结构、噪声特性上差异较大。

Ideas for my research: 1) 借鉴"PLS latent variables作为降维策略"的思路——在遥感目标检测中，可考虑对高光谱/多光谱波段进行特征降维（如PCA/PLS）以减少输入通道，降低模型计算量；2) TabPFN的in-context learning思想可启发小样本遥感目标检测的few-shot方法设计；3) 该论文"显式降维并非必要"的结论提示：在轻量化设计中应权衡特征降维与端到端学习的收益。

Abstract: Visible and near-infrared (vis-NIR) and mid-infrared (MIR) spectroscopy enable rapid, cost-effective prediction of soil properties. Yet, translating high-dimensional, highly collinear spectra into accurate soil property predictions remains challenging, particularly when employing machine learning. We systematically investigated regression models and dimensionality reduction approaches for spectroscopic modeling across 85 regression tasks from open benchmark datasets in pedometrics spanning field-scale digital soil mapping and a global soil spectral library. We compared an in-context learning tabular foundation model (TabPFN), a convolutional neural network (CNN), rule-based regression (Cubist), Random Forest, and partial least squares regression (PLSR) using full spectra as well as features derived from principal component analysis (PCA) and partial least squares (PLS) latent variables. TabPFN consistently delivered the best overall performance across scales, including large spectral library tasks with tens of thousands of soil samples. Notably, TabPFN applied directly to full spectra already surpassed all classical baselines, showing that explicit dimensionality reduction is not strictly required for strong performance. Further improvements were achieved through PLS, which proved to be an effective dimensionality reduction strategy for all models. Combining PLS latent variables with TabPFN yielded the best predictions overall. Our findings provide evidence-based guidance for spectroscopic calibration model selection across operational scales, demonstrating that the long-standing advantages of PLSR and modern tabular foundation models complement each other in chemometrics.

</details>


### [60] [RHEA: Reliability-Harmonized Reconstruction and Assignment for Robust Multimodal-Attributed Graph Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00621)
*Yinlin Zhu,Di Wu,Ziyu Han,Zekai Chenm,Wang Luo,Miao Hu,Guocong Quan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RHEA，一个面向多模态属性图（MAG）聚类的可靠性感知框架，通过邻域共识估计节点级模态可靠性，并在聚类全流程中传播该信号，以应对属性噪声和缺失问题。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2分）。该论文属于泛机器学习/图学习领域，聚焦多模态属性图聚类（community discovery, product segmentation），与遥感、卫星、在轨目标检测、轻量化部署均无直接关联。既非遥感场景，也非目标检测任务，更不涉及模型压缩或部署优化。仅在多模态特征融合与可靠性估计方面可能对遥感多模态数据（如光学+SAR）处理有间接借鉴意义。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向基本无关，既非遥感场景、非目标检测任务，也不涉及任何轻量化或部署技术。仅在多模态可靠性融合的算法思想上可能有极间接的借鉴价值，不建议优先精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：无监督多模态属性图聚类（MAG clustering），包括社区发现和产品分割。应用场景为通用图数据（如社交网络、电商产品），非遥感场景。传感器类型：不适用（节点属性为文本和图像）。目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: RHEA框架包含三个核心模块：(1) 基于邻域共识的节点级模态可靠性估计；(2) 可靠性感知的多模态融合（adaptive weighting）；(3) 拓扑感知的最优传输聚类（topology-aware optimal transport clustering），包含可靠性感知传输分配和邻域共识分配蒸馏。此外，重建表示的置信度被纳入聚类目标函数。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏（注：文中"distillation"指聚类分配蒸馏，非模型压缩中的knowledge distillation）、NAS或轻量网络设计。无任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未提供参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署相关信息。该工作面向离线聚类任务，与星上/边缘部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 使用4个MAG基准数据集，在5种属性条件（不同噪声/缺失程度）下进行实验。评价指标为NMI（Normalized Mutual Information）。

Experiments: RHEA在4个MAG基准的5种属性条件下均优于最强基线，且NMI增益随属性质量恶化而增大，说明其在噪声/缺失属性场景下的鲁棒性优势。

Limitations: (1) 与遥感目标检测和轻量化完全无关，无法直接迁移；(2) 摘要未说明模型复杂度、推理效率；(3) 仅验证了聚类任务，未涉及检测任务；(4) 未说明在真实大规模图数据上的可扩展性；(5) 最优传输聚类通常计算开销较大，可能不适合资源受限场景。

Ideas for my research: (1) 模态可靠性估计思想可借鉴：在遥感多模态数据（如光学+SAR、多光谱+高光谱）融合检测中，不同模态可能因云遮挡、传感器故障而缺失或噪声，可借鉴"邻域共识估计模态可靠性"的思路，对不可靠模态进行自适应降权或重建；(2) 可靠性感知融合策略可迁移到遥感多模态目标检测的特征融合模块；(3) 但需注意，该方法的图结构假设（attribute homophily）在遥感图像中是否成立需另行验证，且最优传输聚类的计算开销需评估。

Abstract: Multimodal-attributed graphs (MAGs), whose nodes carry heterogeneous attributes such as text and images over a relational structure, have become a fundamental substrate for label-free entity grouping tasks, including community discovery and product segmentation. Existing MAG clustering methods effectively integrate complementary modalities when attributes are clean and complete, but degrade substantially under noisy or missing attributes because they implicitly assume equal modality reliability across all nodes. In practice, modality reliability is inherently node-specific: images may be corrupted or absent, while textual descriptions are incomplete or noisy. We argue that, under attribute homophily, graph neighborhoods naturally provide supervision-free evidence for estimating node-specific modality reliability. Based on this insight, we propose RHEA, a reliability-aware framework for MAG clustering that estimates node-specific modality reliability from neighborhood consensus and propagates this signal throughout the clustering pipeline. RHEA reconstructs unreliable or missing modalities from graph neighborhoods, adaptively weights modalities during reliability-aware fusion, and performs topology-aware optimal transport clustering with reliability-aware transport assignment and neighbor-consensus assignment distillation. Furthermore, the confidence of reconstructed representations is incorporated into the clustering objective, allowing uncertain reconstructions to contribute proportionally during optimization. Experiments on four MAG benchmarks under five attribute conditions show that RHEA consistently outperforms the strongest baseline, with NMI gains increasing as attribute quality deteriorates.

</details>

Abstract: A wide range of statistical and machine learning methods have been proposed for survival analysis with competing risks, where the occurrence of one event (i.e., cancer death) precludes the occurrence of other events (i.e., cardiovascular disease death). Despite these methodological advances, their systematic evaluation and adoption are limited by the lack of comprehensive, reproducible and extensible benchmarking frameworks. We developed an open-source benchmarking framework for competing risks models that enables their systematic comparison across multiple datasets under different aspects of performance; calibration, discrimination, overall prediction error and clinical utility. We additionally introduce an extension of SHAP for competing risks, allowing model-agnostic interpretability of covariates contributions over time. All our code is publicly available via GitHub:https://github.com/BBolosSierra/CompRisksBenchmark

### [62] [Causal Inference with Unstructured Treatments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00657)
*Kevin Christian Wibisono,Yixin Wang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一种针对非结构化处理变量（文本、图像、动态治疗序列）的因果推断查询方法——最大影响力特征（MIF），用于识别对结果影响最强的处理特征，并配套提出nudging算法对处理进行修订以改善结果。

Research relevance: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文属于因果推断（causal inference）领域，关注非结构化处理变量（unstructured treatment）的特征影响力分析，应用场景为课程描述、图像、动态治疗序列等，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。相关性评分：1分。

Reading priority: 1分。论文属于因果推断领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入阅读时间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：因果推断（causal inference）中的特征影响力分析。应用场景：课程描述优化（吸引学生）、图像处理、动态治疗序列（clinical decisions）。传感器类型：不适用。目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 提出MIF（maximally influential feature）形式化定义：将处理变量视为由feature-scoring function定义的二元特征，约束两个取值均保持充分填充，并最大化其诱导的因果效应。配套提出nudging algorithm用于沿MIF方向修订处理变量以改善结果。摘要未说明具体网络结构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文不讨论剪枝（pruning）、量化（quantization）、知识蒸馏（knowledge distillation）、NAS、实时推理或边缘部署等任何模型压缩与加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 不适用。论文不涉及星上/边缘部署，未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集。论文在text、image、dynamic treatment sequences三类应用上进行了算法验证，但未给出具体数据集名称、评价指标或实验设置细节。

Experiments: 摘要仅说明在文本、图像和动态治疗序列三类应用中展示了MIF算法，未给出具体实验结果、对比方法或消融实验细节。

Limitations: 摘要未说明局限性。但可推断：MIF依赖feature-scoring function的设计与选择，其因果识别条件（identification conditions）在复杂高维处理变量下可能难以满足；且该方法面向因果推断而非视觉感知任务，与遥感目标检测场景无直接关联。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限。唯一可能的间接启发是：MIF中"识别对结果影响最大的特征"的思想，在遥感目标检测的可解释性分析（如分析哪些特征对检测性能影响最大）或特征重要性评估中或有方法论参考价值，但与本课题的轻量化核心方向关联极弱。

Abstract: Causal inference usually concerns a scalar treatment, yet in many problems the treatment is unstructured: a text, an image, or a sequence of clinical decisions. Consider an instructor writing a course description to attract more students: the treatment is the course description, and the outcome is enrollment. The standard target, the average treatment effect of fixing the treatment to one exact value versus another, runs into two problems. It cannot be estimated, because almost no exact description recurs across courses, leaving no comparable group from which to measure its effect; and it would be of little use even if it could, since no one wants every course to carry the same description. What the instructor actually wants to know is which features of a description raise enrollment, and which of those features can be acted on across many courses. To this end, we propose a causal query for unstructured treatments: the maximally influential feature (MIF), the feature of the treatment that most strongly influences the outcome. We formalize the MIF as a binary feature of the treatment, defined by a feature-scoring function, constrained so that both of its values stay well populated, and chosen to maximize the causal effect it induces. Turning the feature on shifts the distribution of treatments toward those that display it, turning it off shifts away, and the MIF effect contrasts the two average potential outcomes. We study identification conditions for the MIF, develop algorithms to estimate it, and make it actionable through a nudging algorithm that revises a treatment along the MIF into an outcome-improving version. We illustrate the MIF algorithm across applications in text, image, and dynamic treatment sequences.

</details>


### [63] [Round-Trip Consistency: Bidirectional Diffusion Models Can Predict Their Own Rollout Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00675)
*Alexander Scheinker*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一种双向条件潜扩散模型，利用"前向i步再后向i步"的往返一致性误差作为无监督的测试时误差信号，用于自回归/生成式动力学模型的长程rollout误差估计与不确定性量化，无需集成、无标注数据或控制方程。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2分）。该论文属于泛机器学习/生成式建模方向，核心是条件潜扩散模型（conditional latent diffusion model）用于动力学系统（MHD、湍流Navier-Stokes、人脸视频）的时序演化预测与误差自监督估计。它不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱或多光谱数据，也不涉及目标检测任务，更不涉及剪枝/量化/蒸馏/NAS等轻量化方法。其唯一可借鉴点是"往返一致性（round-trip consistency）"作为一种自监督误差信号的思想，可能对遥感时序预测或模型校准有间接启发，但与在轨目标检测及轻量化主线基本无关。

Reading priority: 2分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"主线弱相关。其往返一致性自监督误差估计思想对在轨无真值场景下的模型可靠性评估有间接启发，但论文不涉及遥感、目标检测或任何轻量化技术，且扩散模型推理成本与在轨实时需求冲突，仅建议作为方法论参考快速浏览，不值得深入精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：生成式时序动力学建模（forward/backward rollout）与测试时误差估计/不确定性量化。场景：可压缩磁流体力学（MHD）、天体物理湍流辐射混合层、自然人脸视频（CelebV-HQ）、湍流Navier-Stokes（LE-PDE-UQ基准）。传感器类型：非遥感（物理仿真数据与自然视频）。目标类别：无目标检测概念，涉及物理场（六个解码物理场）与人脸视频帧。

Model architecture: 单一条件潜扩散模型（single conditional latent diffusion model），通过方向标志（direction flag）控制模型沿时间前向或后向步进动力学系统。核心机制是双向rollout：前向i步再后向i步应回到起点，往返差异C_i作为自监督误差代理。训练为双向（bidirectional training），负成本，同时优于单向专家模型。后向方向兼作快速逆求解器。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计、实时推理或边缘/星上部署等模型压缩与加速方法。唯一与"轻量"沾边的是：单个双向模型在LE-PDE-UQ基准上以十分之一的训练成本达到十模型集成约1.3倍内的精度，即用单模型替代集成（ensemble）以降低计算开销，但这属于推理成本优化而非传统轻量化。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性指标。虽然"单模型替代十模型集成"暗示推理成本降低，但未给出任何面向边缘/星上部署的量化数据。扩散模型本身通常推理开销较大，与在轨实时检测的部署需求存在明显张力。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：可压缩MHD轨迹、天体物理湍流辐射混合层、CelebV-HQ（自然人脸视频）、LE-PDE-UQ湍流Navier-Stokes基准、Orszag-Tang涡（OOD测试）。指标：Spearman秩相关（0.91-0.98固定深度；0.69±0.16轨迹内）、校准误差（1.14×@68%、1.29×@95%）、AUROC（0.98，深度10时1.0）、误差削减（80%覆盖率下15%，为depth-only基线3倍）、训练成本（十分之一）。

Experiments: 主要实验：1) 在MHD轨迹上，往返一致性C_i能对rollout误差进行排序（Spearman 0.91-0.98）；2) 简单校准器在训练rollout上拟合可预测误差量级（1.14×@68%）；3) OOD的Orszag-Tang涡上信号能准确标记分布外（AUROC 0.98），而采样离散度基线在此失效；4) 80%覆盖率下削减15%累积误差，为depth-only基线3倍；5) 双向训练负成本，双向模型在两个方向均优于方向专家；6) LE-PDE-UQ上单双向模型以1/10训练成本达到十模型集成1.3倍内精度，且获得最佳无训练像素级校准。

Limitations: 1) 与遥感/卫星/目标检测完全无关，非本方向场景；2) 未涉及任何轻量化技术（剪枝/量化/蒸馏/NAS）；3) 未报告部署相关指标（参数量、FLOPs、延迟、能耗）；4) 扩散模型推理成本通常较高，与在轨实时检测需求冲突；5) 方法针对生成式时序建模与误差估计，不直接适用于目标检测任务；6) 摘要未说明方法在真实遥感数据上的泛化能力。

Ideas for my research: 1) "往返一致性（round-trip consistency）"思想可迁移到遥感时序预测/视频目标检测的误差自监督估计：若检测模型对时序帧做前向-后向推理，往返不一致可作无监督的置信度/不确定性信号，辅助在轨场景下无真值时的检测可靠性评估；2) "单模型替代集成"的思路提示：在轨部署中可用单一轻量模型配合自监督校准信号替代多模型集成，降低推理开销；3) 自监督误差代理（无需标注、无需控制方程）对星上无地面真值的场景有启发，可用于模型漂移监测或OOD检测；4) 但需注意：该方法本身不提供轻量化手段，需结合剪枝/量化/蒸馏等压缩技术才能落地在轨。

Abstract: Autoregressive models accumulate error over long rollouts, yet at deployment there is no ground truth to measure it against. We train a single conditional latent diffusion model that steps a dynamical system forward or backward in time via a direction flag, and show that this bidirectionality supplies a measurement-free test-time error signal: rolling forward $i$ steps and then backward $i$ steps must return the model to its start, so the round-trip discrepancy $\mathcal{C}_i$ is a self-supervised proxy for the unobservable rollout error: no ensembles, no held-out data, no governing equations, for one extra rollout. We validate on compressible magnetohydrodynamics (MHD), an astrophysical turbulent radiative mixing layer, and natural face videos (CelebV-HQ). On held-out MHD trajectories, $\mathcal{C}_i$ ranks rollout error (Spearman $0.91$-$0.98$ at fixed depth; $0.69 \pm 0.16$ within trajectories), and a simple calibrator fit on training rollouts predicts its magnitude to within $1.14\times$ ($68\%$) and $1.29\times$ ($95\%$) with near-nominal coverage - one nat beyond a depth-only predictor, transferring to all six decoded physical fields. The same signal flags the out-of-distribution Orszag-Tang vortex (AUROC $0.98$; $1.0$ by depth $10$) exactly where sampling-dispersion baselines invert, and it cuts incurred error by $15\%$ at $80\%$ coverage - three times the depth-only baseline. Bidirectional training comes at negative cost, beating direction specialists in both directions, and the backward direction doubles as a fast inverse solver. On LE-PDE-UQ's turbulent Navier-Stokes benchmark, a single bidirectional model reaches accuracy within $1.3\times$ of their ten-model ensemble at a tenth of the training cost, with the best training-free pixel-level calibration. Round-trip consistency turns reversibility into a practical trust signal for generative models.

</details>


### [64] [Model-Agnostic FDR Control via Group Gaussian Mirror and Permutation SHAP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00989)
*Jiaan Han,Junxiao Chen,Yanzhe Fu*

Main category: stat.ML

Abstract: Most FDR-controlled feature selection methods are designed for coordinate-wise hypotheses, where each feature has a single weight or importance score. This abstraction fails in sequential and grouped models, where one original feature is represented by a block of sub-features, such as lags, recurrent states, or attention-based interactions. We propose a grouped-feature FDR control framework for such settings. For grouped linear models, we construct null-symmetric block-level mirror statistics with matrix-valued perturbations. For neural sequential models, we combine Permutation SHAP derivatives as model-agnostic block-level importance scores with kernel-based dependence measure. The framework is model-agnostic across network architectures, does not require specifying the covariate distribution, and reduces to Gaussian Mirror or Neural Gaussian Mirror when the block size is one. We prove FDR control for low- and high-dimensional grouped linear models and asymptotic symmetry of smoothed Permutation SHAP derivatives under fixed fitted nonlinear models. Experiments on simulated and real-world datasets show reliable FDR control and improved power under correlated grouped-feature signals.

Abstract: Detecting that a stream of high-dimensional embeddings has changed is usually framed as a choice of statistic. We give a scale law that constrains any moment-based choice and test it against topological alternatives. The law: certifying a feature of spatial scale eps carrying mass fraction f requires polynomial tests of degree N* >= log(1/f)/(2 eps), proved via the Chebyshev extremal problem; a Gauss-quadrature construction gives N* >= 4b-1 for a b-scale topology, so cost is set by feature fineness, not feature count. The law is one-sided: we exhibit an annulus whose mean, covariance and all fourth-order moments equal those of a filled disk, yet H_1 is nonzero.
  Its practical content is a calibration rule. The upper bound is attained by Gaussian test functions, the RKHS witness of an RBF kernel, so the law predicts which bandwidth an MMD test should use: the feature scale. On real embedding streams we measure sigma*/eps with median 1.12 (IQR 1.01-1.52, n=26) over three settings and three scales, and a data-driven bandwidth reaches AUC >= 0.95. Against an adversary optimised against the defender's statistics (mean, covariance, k-NN, kurtosis), only a bandwidth-matched kernel test still detects.
  For persistent homology the verdict is mixed and depends on choices usually left implicit. The summary matters more than the filtration: total persistence attains recall 0.75 at FPR 1% where the first persistence landscape attains 0.00. What survives is a cost gap, not a power gap: where persistence works it costs 116x kurtosis, which works at least as well. We conclude not that topological summaries are useless, but that on this task a kernel test whose bandwidth the law sets dominates them.

</details>

Abstract: We study the problem of identifying the dominant arm in multi-armed bandits, where the objective is to find the action with the highest probability of exceeding the realized rewards of all other actions. Conventional mean-based and pairwise comparison-based algorithms often fail to identify the arm with the highest realized reward. To address this challenge, we introduce a novel dominant arm criterion and an efficient estimator with theoretical guarantees. Our approach relies on two key technical innovations: (i) a dominance score criterion that an arm beats the locally dominant over the partitioned reward space and (ii) a joint mixing and recycling mechanism coupled with a doubly robust estimator that guarantees simultaneous convergence of the empirical distribution functions for all arms. These key innovations pave a way to efficient computation of global arm dominance. Our proposed elimination algorithm identifies the best dominant arm with nearly optimal rate of sample complexity. Numerical experiments demonstrate that our algorithm consistently achieves exact recovery of the true dominant arm, outperforming existing baselines.

</details>

Abstract: Drifting objectives compare a target and model distribution through a vector field observed noisily at finitely many locations. We ask what distributional conclusion such a frozen measurement system warrants. For integrable antisymmetric interactions and absolutely continuous laws in a declared finite density basis, the unnormalized sampled numerator satisfies $\operatorname{vec}(V_X)=Mc$, where $c$ is an antisymmetric mismatch and $M$ is probe-dependent. This identity yields an a posteriori total-variation (TV) upper confidence bound accounting for held-out field noise, estimated-operator error, and externally validated $L^1$ residual radii around normalized density approximants in the span; a nonpositive observability margin returns the trivial TV bound and abstains. The audit recomputes this numerator from held-out samples; a normalized drift statistic requires a separate joint numerator--denominator analysis. For Gaussian-RBF interactions, a global envelope supports distribution-free and empirical-Bernstein radii without truncation, with companion bounds for the Laplace similarity in the original drifting objective. We characterize random-probe observability by a population Gram matrix, identify rank and symmetry degeneracies, and prove large-bandwidth collapse toward mean matching. Synthetic studies exercise Gaussian and Laplace numerators, separately prespecified bounded-vector and variance-adaptive radii, Monte Carlo-calibrated operators, nonzero residual radii around normalized finite-basis approximants, outward-rounded observability bounds, and designed abstention. A joint basis-size/dimension stress path extends evaluation through $m=8$. The result is a conditional diagnostic for a finite density class, or for normalized finite-basis density approximants with external residual radii, not a universal guarantee from small training drift.

</details>

Abstract: Machine-learning benchmarks often pair a label that aggregates a long temporal horizon with input observed through one or a few short windows. Their apparent performance ceiling may therefore be an acquisition-protocol ceiling rather than a model-capacity ceiling. We study labels of the form $Θ_{g,T}=T^{-1}\int_0^T g\{Z(t)\}\,\mathrm{d}t$ when the latent Gaussian process contains both a stable individual trait and a correlated within-individual state. An exact protocol-conditioned Bayes-risk identity provides a common tool. First, we decompose label variance into an $O(1)$ trait component and an $O(T^{-1})$ state component, explaining why a snapshot can retain cross-sectional predictability while poorly tracking within-person change. Second, we derive task-dependent effective temporal spans: mean labels depend on the ordinary correlation time, whereas occupation-time labels depend on an entire spectrum of higher-order correlation times. Third, state-driven occupation-label variance is maximal when the stable trait lies at the threshold; window efficiency decays much more slowly away from that boundary. Under an equal segment budget, exact risks and Monte Carlo experiments show that repeated segments at one time rapidly saturate, whereas temporally dispersed observations continue to increase state explainability. The trait ceiling uses quantities available from ordinary test-retest data; only the state ceiling requires short-lag temporal calibration. The results distinguish architectural limits from protocol limits and show that the label, rather than duration or segment count alone, defines the relevant timescale.

</details>

Abstract: Given an algebraic statistical model, a challenging problem is classifying the data according to the number of positive critical points of the likelihood function. The positive critical points are the positive solutions to an algebraic system, say likelihood equations. So, identifying the number of positive critical points is a real root classification problem for the likelihood equations. A discriminant variety of a likelihood-equation system geometrically describes the data for which the number of real solutions becomes unusual. As an essential component of the discriminant variety, the nonproperness set collects the data such that the likelihood-equation system has a solution at infinity. So, the number of real solutions varies when the data passes the nonproperness set, and identifying the nonproperness set plays a crucial role in the real root classification. In this work, we develop a novel method for computing nonproperness sets of likelihood-equation systems. We prove the correctness of this method. We show experimentally that it is far more efficient than the known methods in the literature.

</details>

Abstract: With AI systems gaining more access to individuals' information, it is important to protect privacy when reporting statistical answers. Equally important is to privatize the reporting of uncertainty in such answers. To this end, we adopt a Bayesian likelihood-free framework and make simulation from the posterior private. In particular, we propose a new private instantiation of the Bayesian bootstrap using a blocking strategy. Rather than assigning idiosyncratic random weights to each individual, we randomly group individuals and assign a single weight to each group. By concealing individuals' contributions within a group, we fortify differential privacy gates. We harness amortized inference that decouples private learning from posterior sampling. A push-forward map from observation weights to posterior samples is learned privately by adding calibrated noise during training. Subsequent posterior draws require no additional privacy and computation budget. We call the resulting method the Private Generative Bayesian Bootstrap (PGBB). We establish a differential privacy guarantee, analyze convergence to the non-private blocked-bootstrap target, and quantify the discrepancy between the ordinary and blocked Bayesian-bootstrap posteriors. In addition, we derive data-free tuning of the block Dirichlet concentration parameter that restores posterior dispersion asymptotically. We also show a single fit of PGBB can support a family of loss-based decision rules simultaneously without additional privacy cost. In simulations and in applications to U.S. Census returns to schooling and U.S. natality birthweight quantiles, PGBB gives competitive private uncertainty quantification and improves over private Bayesian alternatives that require a specified data-generating model in common settings.

</details>

Abstract: Recently, rectified flow has emerged as a fundamental framework for large-scale image generation, powering state-of-the-art systems such as FLUX.1 and Stable Diffusion 3. Despite its remarkable empirical success, the computational and statistical guarantees of iterative rectified flow have remained largely unexplored. We address this problem by studying \textit{c}-rectified flow, a cost-aware class of rectified flow that projects velocity fields onto a gradient class while preserving endpoint marginals. The ordinary rectified flow can fail to recover the optimal transport coupling: in a Gaussian case study, the iteration converges to the optimal coupling if and only if the source and target covariance matrices commute. In contrast, under suitable compactness and uniform-integrability assumptions, iterative \textit{c}-rectified flow always converges to the optimal transport coupling. We further establish quantitative one-step contraction and exponential convergence guarantees under projection-stability assumptions for both quadratic and strongly convex displacement costs. Finally, under a Hölder ball assumption, we develop new minimax-optimal score estimation rates and show that, when combined with iterative \textit{c}-rectified flow, they yield a rate-optimal estimator of the optimal transport for the dimension \(d \ge 3\) and a nearly parametric rate for \(d=1,2\).

</details>

Abstract: This paper is concerned with one-bit mean estimation, where each independent sample is represented by a single binary message. We consider distributions on $\mathbb{R}$ with mean in $[-λ,λ]$ and absolute $k$-th central moment at most $σ^k$, where $k>1$ is fixed. For this class, previous work attained the optimal sample complexity for general queries using a two-stage protocol. The first stage localizes the mean. The second-stage queries are chosen after localization and refine the estimate around the decoded center. We show that this interaction can be avoided by constructing a randomized fully non-adaptive protocol that fixes all queries before observing the data and matches the optimal adaptive sample complexity. For target accuracy $ε$ and confidence $1-δ$, its sample complexity scales as \[ \log\fracλσ +
\begin{cases} (σ/ε)^2\log(1/δ), & k>2,\\ (σ/ε)^2\log(σ/ε)\log(1/δ), & k=2,\\ (σ/ε)^{k/(k-1)}\log(1/δ), & 1<k<2, \end{cases} \] up to constants depending only on $k$. In the range covered by the known lower bound, this rate is minimax optimal even among fully adaptive protocols. This gives a negative answer to the COLT 2026 open problem asking whether interaction is necessary for order-optimal one-bit mean estimation with general queries \citep[Open Problem~1]{lau2026open}.

</details>

### [73] [Sensor Deployment Optimization for Passive TDOA Localization Under Unknown Drift Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00034)
*Zhenxing Zhang,Tianxian Zhang,Zerui Zhang,Zicheng Wang,Xueting Li*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究传感器离线部署优化问题，提出基于漂移误差的广义几何精度因子（GDOP_D）和自适应单向粒子群优化器（AUPSO），用于在传感器位置存在漂移误差时实现稳健的TDOA定位精度。

Abstract: This paper investigates how to deploy sensors offline to provide robust passive TDOA localization accuracy across the entire region of interest (ROI) when their positions are subject to drift errors caused by factors such as wind. Since in practice only the 1st and 2nd order statistics of sensor drift errors can be estimated from historical sensor telemetry data or wind field statistics, by using them we first derive a generalized geometric dilution of precision under drift errors ($\mathrm{GDOP_{D}}$), which extends the traditional GDOP ($\mathrm{GDOP_{T}}$). Furthermore, we derive theoretical results related to $\mathrm{GDOP_{D}}$ and $\mathrm{GDOP_{T}}$, revealing that drift errors not only enlarge the value of GDOP but also reshape its distribution, thereby degrading localization performance. Then, we construct a $\operatorname{{GDOP}_{D}}$-based min-max deployment optimization problem. {Finally, we propose an adaptive unidirectional particle swarm optimizer (AUPSO) to solve this challenging problem. The proposed method alleviates the premature convergence and the oscillatory behavior of the traditional PSO. Extensive simulations demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method. This research provides a reliable offline sensor deployment planning framework for practical engineering scenarios, when the accurate drift error probability density function is not available.}

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。仅提及使用ADASCA Deep Learning方法作为ATR模型，但未给出其网络结构、层数、模块设计等细节。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明ATR模型的参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署信息。仅提及模拟器Salsa在单张Nvidia GeForce RTX 4090 GPU上的性能：10分钟内生成21,600张合成图像，4小时内生成648,000张图像。但这些是数据生成速度，与星上部署无关。论文未讨论模型在星上/边缘设备的部署可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：MSTAR公开数据集（SAR目标识别标准基准）。评价指标：accuracy（准确率）。实验设置：使用Salsa生成的合成SAR图像训练ATR模型，在MSTAR上评估。主要结果：ADASCA方法达到86%准确率；考虑电磁耦合变异性后达到87%准确率。

Experiments: 主要实验包括：(1) 计算性能测试：Salsa在RTX 4090上10分钟内生成21,600张合成图像；(2) 数据代表性验证：合成数据训练的ATR模型在MSTAR上达到86%准确率（state-of-the-art）；(3) 电磁耦合研究：未考虑地面表面电磁耦合变异性时，模型准确率下降超过4%；(4) 大规模数据生成：4小时内生成648,000张含多种耦合变异的图像，使模型准确率提升至87%。

Limitations: 摘要未说明：(1) ATR模型的具体架构和参数量；(2) 模型推理速度、计算复杂度等部署指标；(3) 与真实SAR数据训练的模型性能对比细节；(4) 合成数据与真实数据之间的domain gap问题；(5) 模型轻量化相关内容完全缺失。此外，MSTAR数据集规模较小且为经典基准，其结论在更复杂、更大规模的真实SAR场景中的泛化性有待验证。

Ideas for my research: 对本课题的借鉴点：(1) 合成数据训练思路：在轨目标检测模型常面临真实SAR/光学遥感标注数据稀缺的问题，可借鉴Salsa模拟器思路，用仿真数据扩充训练集，提升模型在轨泛化能力；(2) 环境变异性鲁棒性研究：论文揭示目标与环境的电磁耦合变异性会显著影响模型性能（准确率下降>4%），这提醒在轨检测模型需要考虑传感器成像条件、地面背景等环境因素的变化；(3) 数据生成效率与模型训练的关系：模拟器在速度和资源消耗上的权衡思路，可类比到在轨场景中轻量化模型对计算资源的约束；(4) 但需注意：论文未涉及模型压缩，若要在轨部署，仍需结合pruning、quantization、knowledge distillation等轻量化手段。

Abstract: This work focuses on training Automatic Target Recognition (ATR) models using simulated Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) images to circumvent the lack of real measurements. To obtain robust and versatile ATR models, simulation needs to generate massive datasets that encompass all the variability found in real measurements. Thus, we need a simulator that finds a good tradeoff between execution speed, computational resource consumption, and physical representativeness. In this work, we demonstrate that the Salsa simulator addresses this issue. We ran computing performance tests to show that Salsa can generate 21,600 synthetic images in less than 10 minutes using a single Nvidia GeForce RTX 4090 GPU. Using our ADASCA Deep Learning approach, we demonstrate that these data are sufficiently representative to train ATR models and reach state-of-the-art results on the MSTAR public dataset with an accuracy of 86 %. To illustrate how Salsa unlocks new possibilities to train ATR models, we use the simulator to conduct a study on Electromagnetic (EM) couplings between the targets and their immediate environment. We demonstrate that, if not accounted for in the training dataset, the variability of the EM couplings induced by the variability of the ground surfaces can significantly degrade the performance of ATR models, with an accuracy decrease of more than 4 %. We also show that Salsa can generate in a timely manner (i.e., in less than 4 hours using the same GPU as previously) a massive dataset of 648,000 images with a large variety of couplings to make the ATR models robust to EM coupling variations. Our ATR models can then achieve an accuracy of 87 % on the MSTAR dataset.

</details>

Model architecture: 提出STF-HFNet（spatiotemporal-frequency hybrid fusion network），包含三个核心模块：1) Frequency aggregation processing——学习自适应频率聚合以建模非平稳动态；2) Reciprocal guided attention——无同步先验的跨模态双向校准；3) Hybrid collaborative fusion——结合空间与通道注意力增强跨模态互补性并抑制冗余信息。属于多模态融合网络，与遥感检测网络架构无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。且该任务面向生理信号情绪识别，与星上部署场景无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：1) 自建多模态嗅觉情绪数据集（111名受试者，气味在2D arousal-valence空间标注，同步记录EEG/ECG/PPG）；2) AMIGOS公开数据集。评价指标：识别准确率（accuracy）。在AMIGOS上达到88.34%，自建数据集上达到92.40%，分别超过SOTA方法8.27%和5.07%。

Abstract: Olfaction is important for emotion regulation because it acts as a non-intrusive and cognitively lightweight pathway that directly engages the brain s affective circuitry and achieves unobtrusive emotional modulation. This trait is essential for advancing practical affective computing in daily and attention-critical scenarios. However, current olfactory emotion research has two key limitations. First, it overemphasises the valence dimension while neglecting arousal. Second, it lacks multimodal datasets that synchronously capture central and peripheral physiological responses to olfactory stimuli. To address these issues, we construct a large-scale multimodal olfactory emotion dataset based on 111 subjects, in which odors are labeled in the 2D arousal-valence space and electroencephalogram (EEG), electrocardiogram (ECG), and photoplethysmography (PPG) signals synchronously recorded. Nevertheless, multimodal signals present challenges such as non-stationarity, differences in latency, and cross-modal heterogeneity. Thus, we propose a spatiotemporal-frequency hybrid fusion network (STF-HFNet), which integrates three core modules. Frequency aggregation processing learns adaptive frequency aggregation in order to model non-stationary dynamics. Reciprocal guided attention enables reciprocal bidirectional calibration for cross-modal temporal alignment without synchronisation priors. Hybrid collaborative fusion combines spatial and channel attention mechanisms to enhance cross-modal complementarity while suppressing redundant information. Extensive experiments show that STF-HFNet achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) recognition accuracies of 88.34% on the AMIGOS dataset and 92.40% on our self-constructed dataset, and outperform the SOTA methods by 8.27% and 5.07%, respectively.

</details>

Abstract: We propose a retrieval-based framework for crossdomain quality-of-transmission (QoT) estimation that leverages transferable feature representations while avoiding reliance on source-domain-specific decision boundaries. The proposed approach supports both zero-shot and few-shot adaptation without requiring model retraining. Experimental results on cross-domain QoT datasets demonstrate improved generalization performance compared with conventional machine learning baselines and recent contrastive learning approaches, highlighting the potential of retrieval-based inference for robust optical network automation.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：EEG脑电信号生成（data augmentation / generative modeling）。应用场景：脑机接口（Brain Computer Interface, BCI）应用中的数据增强与大规模神经建模。传感器类型：EEG（脑电图，非遥感传感器）。目标类别：不涉及目标检测，生成对象为EEG信号样本。

Model architecture: 基于条件流匹配（conditional flow matching）的生成框架。关键模块包括：(1) Position-Adaptive Time Scheduling——根据每个位置的重构误差调制流匹配轨迹中位置特定的时间进度；(2) Factorized Spatio-Temporal Attention——因子化时空注意力，建模由volume conduction引起的跨通道依赖；(3) frequency-aligned multi-resolution spectral consistency loss——频率对齐的多分辨率谱一致性损失，补偿EEG的power law谱偏置。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文未涉及任何模型压缩或加速方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署相关信息，且EEG生成任务本身与星上/边缘部署场景无关。

Datasets and metrics: 使用三个EEG数据集，具有不同的采集协议（acquisition protocols）和任务语义（task semantics）。评价指标包括：TS-FID（时间序列Fréchet Inception Distance，降低最多62.2%）和下游分类准确率提升（最多提升6.77个百分点）。

Experiments: 在三个EEG数据集上与最强基线（strongest baseline）对比，所提框架一致优于基线：TS-FID最多降低62.2%，下游分类准确率增益最多提升6.77个百分点。摘要未说明具体对比方法名称和消融实验细节。

Limitations: (1) 与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化方向基本无关，属于脑机接口领域的信号生成任务；(2) 摘要未报告模型复杂度、推理速度等部署指标；(3) 摘要未说明生成样本的多样性、真实性验证细节；(4) 未说明方法在非EEG信号（如图像、遥感数据）上的泛化能力；(5) 摘要未提及任何轻量化或边缘部署策略。

Ideas for my research: 尽管论文与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向弱相关，但仍有少量可借鉴的泛化思路：(1) "位置自适应时间调度"（Position-Adaptive Time Scheduling）中根据每个位置的重构误差动态调制训练进度的思想，可类比迁移到遥感目标检测中针对不同尺度/不同难度的目标（如小目标）设计自适应训练策略或损失加权；(2) "因子化时空注意力"（Factorized Spatio-Temporal Attention）将高维注意力分解为空间和时间两个维度，这种分解思想可用于降低遥感图像中高分辨率特征图上的注意力计算复杂度，从而辅助轻量化设计；(3) 多分辨率谱一致性损失的思想可类比为遥感检测中多尺度特征一致性约束。但总体而言，这些借鉴点较为间接，论文本身不提供任何遥感场景或轻量化部署的直接经验。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) generation is essential for alleviating data scarcity and enabling large scale neural modeling in brain computer interface applications. However, existing flow based approaches assume that every channel and every time segment within a sample shares a single global time progression, overlooking the fact that not all EEG moments are equal. To address this overlooked heterogeneity, we propose an adaptive EEG generation framework built on conditional flow matching. The framework introduces Position-Adaptive Time Scheduling, which tracks per position reconstruction error to modulate a position specific time progress within the flow matching trajectory. It further incorporates Factorized Spatio-Temporal Attention and a frequency aligned multi resolution spectral consistency loss to model inter channel dependencies induced by volume conduction and compensate for the power law spectral bias of EEG, thereby improving the quality of generated signals. Extensive experiments on three EEG datasets with distinct acquisition protocols and task semantics show that our framework consistently outperforms the strongest baseline, reducing TS-FID by up to 62.2\% and improving downstream classification accuracy gain by up to 6.77 percentage points. These results suggest that the proposed method represents a promising step toward scalable, high fidelity data augmentation for real world brain computer interface applications.

</details>


### [78] [Identifiability-Aware Source Apportionment in City-Scale Advection-Diffusion Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00050)
*Ankit Bhardwaj,Lakshminarayanan Subramanian*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种基于可辨识性感知的源解析框架（IASA），用于从稀疏城市空气质量传感器数据中反演污染源贡献，通过低维非负时间基和风条件滞后逆问题实现源归因与不确定性量化。
